+//! Process & System Call Management / Surec ve Sistem cagrilari Yonetimi.
+//!
+//! This module:
+//! 1. Process Control Block (PCB),
+//! 2. Process states (Ready, Running, Blocked, Terminated),
+//! 3. x86_64 Register/Context structure and process memory/resource tracking,
+//! 4. Process Table: Process spawn/exec, terminate (exit/kill),
+//! 5. System calls (Syscalls): `sys_yield`, `sys_exit`, `sys_write`, `sys_read`,
+//!    `sys_alloc_frame`, `sys_free_frame`, `sys_get_pid`, `sys_time`, etc.
+//! 6. Separate virtual address space (AddressSpace / Page Table) management per process.
+//!
+//! Bu modul:
+//! 1. Surec Kontrol Blogu (PCB - Process Control Block),
+//! 2. Surec durumlari (Hazir, calisiyor, Uyuyor, Sonlandi),
+//! 3. x86_64 Register/Context yapisi ve surec bellek/kaynak takibi,
+//! 4. Surec tablosu (Process Table): Surec baslatma (spawn/exec), sonlandirma (exit/kill),
+//! 5. Sistem cagrilari (Syscalls): `sys_yield`, `sys_exit`, `sys_write`, `sys_read`,
+//!    `sys_alloc_frame`, `sys_free_frame`, `sys_get_pid`, `sys_time` vb.
+//! 6. Her surec icin ayri sanal adres uzayi (AddressSpace / Page Table) yonetimi.
+
+use crate::mm::fd::{self, FdTable, FileDescriptor, FileKind, FileTable};
+use crate::mm::pmm::{PhysicalMemoryManager, PAGE_SIZE};
+use crate::mm::uaccess;
+use crate::mm::vmm::{AddressSpace, PageFlags};
+
+/// Azami eszamanli aktif surec sayisi
+pub const MAX_PROCESSES: usize = 32;
+
+/// ├ç├Âz├╝len tembel (demand paging) sayfa hatas─▒ sayac─▒ (te┼şhis).
+pub static LAZY_FAULTS: core::sync::atomic::AtomicU64 = core::sync::atomic::AtomicU64::new(0);
+
+/// Copy-on-write yaz─▒l─▒m biti: x86_64 mimarisi bit 9'u (Ignore) yok sayar,
+/// bu y├╝zden ayn─▒ ├Ânbellek hatt─▒n─▒ payla┼ş─▒mayan g├╝venli bir yaz─▒l─▒m biti olarak
+/// serbest kullan─▒labilir. Fork, yaz─▒labilir sayfalar─▒ iki tarafta da bu
+/// bayrakla salt-okunur b─▒rak─▒r; ilk yazma #PF ├╝retir ve `handle_cow_fault`
+/// sayfay─▒ ├Âzel kopyaya d├Ân├╝┼şt├╝r├╝r.
+pub const PAGE_COW: u64 = 1 << 9;
+
+/// Tembel (demand) sayfa yaz─▒l─▒m biti (AVL / PTE bit 10).
+///
+/// `PRESENT` + `PAGE_LAZY` olan bir kullan─▒c─▒ PTE'si payla┼ş─▒lan s─▒f─▒r
+/// sayfas─▒ ├ğer├ğevesini g├Âsterir ve yaz─▒labilir de─şildir; sayfa i├ğin ├Âzel
+/// fiziksel ├ğer├ğeve hen├╝z ayr─▒lmam─▒┼şt─▒r. ─░lk yazma koruma ihlali `#PF`
+/// ├╝retir ve `handle_lazy_fault` s─▒f─▒rlanm─▒┼ş bir ├ğer├ğeve ay─▒r─▒p PTE'yi
+/// kal─▒c─▒ bayraklarla de─şi┼ştirir. x86_64 bu biti yok sayar, dolay─▒s─▒yla
+/// PTE ge├ğerlili─şi bozulmaz.
+pub const PAGE_LAZY: u64 = 1 << 10;
+
+/// Surec Durumu
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum ProcessState {
+    Unused,
+    Ready,
+    Running,
+    Blocked,
+    /// SIGSTOP ile duraklat─▒lm─▒┼ş s├╝re├ğ: ba─şlam─▒ kay─▒tl─▒ kal─▒r, zamanlay─▒c─▒
+    /// onu se├ğmez; SIGCONT ile `Ready`'ye d├Âner.
+    Suspended,
+    Terminated,
+}
+
+/// Surec onceligi
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum ProcessPriority {
+    Low = 0,
+    Normal = 1,
+    High = 2,
+    Realtime = 3,
+}
+
+/// CPU Yazmac/Baglam Durumu (Saved Context on Interrupt/Syscall)
+#[repr(C)]
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
+pub struct ProcessContext {
+    pub rax: u64,
+    pub rbx: u64,
+    pub rcx: u64,
+    pub rdx: u64,
+    pub rsi: u64,
+    pub rdi: u64,
+    pub rbp: u64,
+    pub rsp: u64,
+    pub r8: u64,
+    pub r9: u64,
+    pub r10: u64,
+    pub r11: u64,
+    pub r12: u64,
+    pub r13: u64,
+    pub r14: u64,
+    pub r15: u64,
+    pub rip: u64,
+    pub cs: u64,
+    pub rflags: u64,
+    pub ss: u64,
+    pub cr3: u64,
+    /// PCID (Process-Context Identifier). Kernel i├ğin 0, user s├╝re├ğler i├ğin atanan de─şer.
+    /// TR / EN: PCID for this process context. Kernel = 0, user processes get assigned.
+    pub pcid: u16,
+    /// S├╝recin kendi ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ ├╝zerinde, kay─▒tl─▒ yazma├ğ alan─▒n─▒n
+    /// ba┼şlang─▒c─▒n─▒ g├Âsteren RSP de─şeri. Timer/syscall kesmesinin haz─▒rlad─▒─ş─▒
+    /// `frame` i┼şaret├ğisi buraya yaz─▒l─▒r; `resume_user_frame` bu adresten
+    /// yazma├ğlar─▒ ve `iretq` ├ğer├ğevesini geri y├╝kler.
+    pub kernel_rsp: u64,
+}
+
+impl ProcessContext {
+    /// Mevcut CPU ba─şlam─▒n─▒ kaydeder ve bu yap─▒ya doldurur.
+    /// Genellikle interrupt veya syscall s─▒ras─▒nda ├ğa─şr─▒l─▒r.
+    ///
+    /// # Safety
+    /// Kesme veya syscall ba─şlam─▒nda ├ğa─şr─▒lmal─▒d─▒r; `options(nostack)`
+    /// kulland─▒─ş─▒ndan y─▒─ş─▒n gereksinimi d├╝┼ş├╝kt├╝r ancak ba─şlam─▒n tutarl─▒
+    /// olmas─▒ ├ğa─ş─▒ran─▒n sorumlulu─şundad─▒r.
+    pub unsafe fn save_current() -> Self {
+        let mut ctx = Self::default();
+        core::arch::asm!(
+            "mov {rax}, rax",
+            "mov {rbx}, rbx",
+            "mov {rcx}, rcx",
+            "mov {rdx}, rdx",
+            "mov {rsi}, rsi",
+            "mov {rdi}, rdi",
+            "mov {rbp}, rbp",
+            "mov {rsp}, rsp",
+            "mov {r8}, r8",
+            "mov {r9}, r9",
+            "mov {r10}, r10",
+            "mov {r11}, r11",
+            "mov {r12}, r12",
+            "mov {r13}, r13",
+            "mov {r14}, r14",
+            "mov {r15}, r15",
+            "lea {rip}, [rip + 1]", // Sonraki instruction adresi
+            "pushfq",
+            "pop {rflags}",
+            "mov {cr3}, cr3",
+            rax = out(reg) ctx.rax,
+            rbx = out(reg) ctx.rbx,
+            rcx = out(reg) ctx.rcx,
+            rdx = out(reg) ctx.rdx,
+            rsi = out(reg) ctx.rsi,
+            rdi = out(reg) ctx.rdi,
+            rbp = out(reg) ctx.rbp,
+            rsp = out(reg) ctx.rsp,
+            r8 = out(reg) ctx.r8,
+            r9 = out(reg) ctx.r9,
+            r10 = out(reg) ctx.r10,
+            r11 = out(reg) ctx.r11,
+            r12 = out(reg) ctx.r12,
+            r13 = out(reg) ctx.r13,
+            r14 = out(reg) ctx.r14,
+            r15 = out(reg) ctx.r15,
+            rip = out(reg) ctx.rip,
+            rflags = out(reg) ctx.rflags,
+            cr3 = out(reg) ctx.cr3,
+            options(nostack, preserves_flags),
+        );
+        ctx.pcid = (ctx.cr3 & 0xFFF) as u16;
+        ctx
+    }
+
+    /// Bu ba─şlam─▒ CPU'ya geri y├╝kler ve s├╝reci devam ettirir.
+    /// CR3 de─şi┼şikli─şi ve register geri y├╝kleme i├ğerir.
+    ///
+    /// # Safety
+    /// `self` ge├ğerli bir `ProcessContext` olmal─▒d─▒r; `cr3` alan─▒ ge├ğerli,
+    /// e┼şlenmi┼ş bir PML4 fiziksel adresine i┼şaret etmelidir. ├ça─şr─▒ sonras─▒
+    /// eski y─▒─ş─▒na d├Ân├╝lemez.
+    pub unsafe fn restore(&self) {
+        let cr3 = self.cr3 | (self.pcid as u64);
+        core::arch::asm!(
+            "mov cr3, {cr3}",
+            "mov rax, {rax}",
+            "mov rbx, {rbx}",
+            "mov rcx, {rcx}",
+            "mov rdx, {rdx}",
+            "mov rsi, {rsi}",
+            "mov rdi, {rdi}",
+            "mov rbp, {rbp}",
+            "mov rsp, {rsp}",
+            "mov r8, {r8}",
+            "mov r9, {r9}",
+            "mov r10, {r10}",
+            "mov r11, {r11}",
+            "mov r12, {r12}",
+            "mov r13, {r13}",
+            "mov r14, {r14}",
+            "mov r15, {r15}",
+            "push {rflags}",
+            "popfq",
+            "jmp {rip}",
+            cr3 = in(reg) cr3,
+            rax = in(reg) self.rax,
+            rbx = in(reg) self.rbx,
+            rcx = in(reg) self.rcx,
+            rdx = in(reg) self.rdx,
+            rsi = in(reg) self.rsi,
+            rdi = in(reg) self.rdi,
+            rbp = in(reg) self.rbp,
+            rsp = in(reg) self.rsp,
+            r8 = in(reg) self.r8,
+            r9 = in(reg) self.r9,
+            r10 = in(reg) self.r10,
+            r11 = in(reg) self.r11,
+            r12 = in(reg) self.r12,
+            r13 = in(reg) self.r13,
+            r14 = in(reg) self.r14,
+            r15 = in(reg) self.r15,
+            rflags = in(reg) self.rflags,
+            rip = in(reg) self.rip,
+            options(noreturn, nostack),
+        );
+    }
+}
+
+/// Bir surece tahsis edilen kaynaklar (Sayfa cerceveleri, dosya taniticilari vb.)
+pub const MAX_PROC_FRAMES: usize = 64;
+pub const MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES: usize = 32;
+/// S├╝re├ğ ba┼ş─▒na azami mmap bayt─▒ (64 MiB).
+pub const MAX_PROC_MMAP_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
+/// S├╝re├ğ ba┼ş─▒na azami heap bayt─▒ (offset; `user_mem::HEAP_LIMIT` ile s─▒n─▒rlan─▒r).
+pub const MAX_PROC_HEAP_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
+
+/// S├╝re├ğ ba┼ş─▒na kaynak limitleri / per-process resource limits.
+///
+/// Limitler `fork` ile devral─▒n─▒r, `exec` sonras─▒ korunur ve her tahsis
+/// yolunda (`record_frame`, `record_user_frame`, `mmap`, `brk`, `open`)
+/// zorunlu olarak denetlenir. Kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝re├ğleri `SYS_RESINFO` ile kullan─▒m
+/// ve limitlerini okuyabilir.
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct ResourceLimits {
+    /// Kaynak defterindeki toplam fiziksel ├ğer├ğeve (kernel y─▒─ş─▒n─▒, PML4, imaj).
+    pub max_frames: usize,
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ verisi i├ğin ayr─▒lan ├ğer├ğeve (heap, mmap, COW kopyalar─▒).
+    pub max_user_frames: usize,
+    /// A├ğ─▒k dosya tan─▒t─▒c─▒s─▒ say─▒s─▒ (0/1/2 dahil).
+    pub max_fds: usize,
+    /// Toplam mmap b├Âlge bayt─▒.
+    pub max_mmap_bytes: u64,
+    /// Heap ucunun `HEAP_BASE` g├Âreli azami de─şeri.
+    pub max_heap_bytes: u64,
+}
+
+impl ResourceLimits {
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝re├ğleri i├ğin g├╝venli varsay─▒lanlar.
+    pub const fn user_default() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            max_frames: MAX_PROC_FRAMES,
+            max_user_frames: MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES,
+            max_fds: fd::MAX_FDS,
+            max_mmap_bytes: MAX_PROC_MMAP_BYTES,
+            max_heap_bytes: MAX_PROC_HEAP_BYTES,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// S─▒n─▒rs─▒z (├ğekirdek s├╝reci / PID 0) limitler.
+    pub const fn unlimited() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            max_frames: usize::MAX,
+            max_user_frames: usize::MAX,
+            max_fds: fd::MAX_FDS,
+            max_mmap_bytes: u64::MAX,
+            max_heap_bytes: u64::MAX,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
+pub struct ProcessResources {
+    /// Surecin sahip oldugu tahsis edilmis fiziksel sayfa cerceveleri
+    pub allocated_frames: [u64; MAX_PROC_FRAMES],
+    pub frame_count: usize,
+    pub user_allocated_frames: [u64; MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES],
+    pub user_frame_count: usize,
+    /// Surec tarafindan acilan dosya veya pencere taniticisi (Handle)
+    pub window_idx: Option<usize>,
+    pub app_id: usize,
+    /// Bu s├╝recin kaynak limitleri.
+    pub limits: ResourceLimits,
+}
+
+impl ProcessResources {
+    pub const fn empty() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            allocated_frames: [0; MAX_PROC_FRAMES],
+            frame_count: 0,
+            user_allocated_frames: [0; MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES],
+            user_frame_count: 0,
+            window_idx: None,
+            app_id: 0,
+            limits: ResourceLimits::user_default(),
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn record_frame(&mut self, frame: u64) -> bool {
+        if self.frame_count >= self.limits.max_frames.min(MAX_PROC_FRAMES)
+            || self.allocated_frames[..self.frame_count].contains(&frame)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+        self.allocated_frames[self.frame_count] = frame;
+        self.frame_count += 1;
+        true
+    }
+
+    pub fn take_frame(&mut self, frame: u64) -> bool {
+        let Some(index) = self.allocated_frames[..self.frame_count]
+            .iter()
+            .position(|owned| *owned == frame)
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        self.frame_count -= 1;
+        self.allocated_frames[index] = self.allocated_frames[self.frame_count];
+        self.allocated_frames[self.frame_count] = 0;
+        true
+    }
+
+    pub fn record_user_frame(&mut self, frame: u64) -> bool {
+        if self.user_frame_count >= self.limits.max_user_frames.min(MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES)
+            || !self.record_frame(frame)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+        self.user_allocated_frames[self.user_frame_count] = frame;
+        self.user_frame_count += 1;
+        true
+    }
+
+    pub fn take_user_frame(&mut self, frame: u64) -> bool {
+        let Some(index) = self.user_allocated_frames[..self.user_frame_count]
+            .iter()
+            .position(|owned| *owned == frame)
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        if !self.take_frame(frame) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        self.user_frame_count -= 1;
+        self.user_allocated_frames[index] = self.user_allocated_frames[self.user_frame_count];
+        self.user_allocated_frames[self.user_frame_count] = 0;
+        true
+    }
+}
+
+/// Surec Kontrol Blogu (PCB - Process Control Block)
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
+pub struct Process {
+    pub pid: usize,
+    pub ppid: usize,
+    pub name: [u8; 32],
+    pub name_len: usize,
+    pub state: ProcessState,
+    pub priority: ProcessPriority,
+    pub context: ProcessContext,
+    pub resources: ProcessResources,
+    pub address_space: AddressSpace,
+    /// S├╝rece ├Âzel ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ (Ring3ÔåÆRing0 ge├ği┼şlerinde TSS.rsp0).
+    /// ─░lk kullan─▒c─▒ giri┼şinde tembel (lazy) olarak tahsis edilir.
+    pub kstack_phys: u64,
+    /// ├çekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒n─▒n ├╝st sanal adresi (HHDM ├╝zerinden, 16 bayt hizal─▒).
+    pub kstack_top: u64,
+    /// Kaynaklar─▒ (sayfa ├ğer├ğeveleri + sayfa tablolar─▒) iade edildi mi?
+    pub resources_reaped: bool,
+    pub cpu_time_ticks: u64,
+    pub exit_code: i32,
+    pub has_saved_user_context: bool,
+    /// Olay/timeout ├╝zerine engellenen surecin bekledigi olay anahtari (None = calistirilir).
+    pub wait_event: Option<usize>,
+    /// block_until ile kaydedilen mutlak sonrasi (ms). Dolduysa zamanlayici uyandirir.
+    pub wait_deadline: Option<u64>,
+    pub waiting_child: Option<usize>,
+    pub wait_status_addr: u64,
+    /// `brk` ile y├Ânetilen heap ucunun `mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE` g├Âreli boyu.
+    pub heap_brk: u64,
+    /// ASLR ile se├ğilmi┼ş mmap taban─▒ (s├╝re├ğ ├Âmr├╝ boyunca sabit).
+    pub mmap_base: u64,
+    /// mmap b├Âlgelerinin taban g├Âreli toplam boyu.
+    pub mmap_used: u64,
+    /// A├ğ─▒k mmap b├Âlgeleri.
+    pub mmap_regions: crate::mm::user_mem::MmapTable,
+    /// S├╝re├ğ dosya tan─▒t─▒c─▒s─▒ tablosu (0/1/2 standart, 3+ `open`).
+    pub fds: FdTable,
+    /// Hen├╝z teslim edilmemi┼ş fatal-olmayan sinyallerin bit maskesi.
+    /// Bit `sig - 1` set ise `sig` pending'dir; `kill` ile i┼şlenir, kullan─▒c─▒
+    /// i┼şleyicisine teslimat sonraki sinyal altyap─▒s─▒ maddesinde yap─▒l─▒r.
+    /// `fork`/`exec` ile s─▒f─▒rlan─▒r (POSIX semanti─şi).
+    pub pending_signals: u64,
+    /// S├╝re├ğ grubu kimli─şi (POSIX `pgid`). `setsid`/`setpgid` ile de─şi┼şir.
+    pub pgid: usize,
+    /// Oturum kimli─şi (POSIX `sid`). Oturum lideri `sid == pid` olur.
+    pub sid: usize,
+    /// CPU yak─▒nl─▒k maskesi: bit `i` set ise s├╝re├ğ CPU `i` ├╝zerinde ├ğal─▒┼şabilir.
+    /// `0` t├╝m ├ğevrimi├ği CPU'lar anlam─▒na gelir (varsay─▒lan).
+    pub affinity: u64,
+    /// Sinyal ba┼ş─▒na kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyici adresi (`0` = varsay─▒lan eylem).
+    /// ─░ndeks `sig - 1`; `SIGKILL`/`SIGSTOP` i├ğin i┼şleyici kaydedilemez.
+    pub signal_handlers: [u64; signals::MAX_SIGNAL as usize],
+    /// Engellenen (bloklanan) sinyallerin bit maskesi.
+    pub signal_mask: u64,
+    /// Seccomp-tarz─▒ syscall izin maskesi: bit `n` set ise `n` numaral─▒
+    /// syscall ├ğa─şr─▒labilir. `u64::MAX` = filtresiz (varsay─▒lan).
+    /// `SYS_SECCOMP` ile yaln─▒zca daralt─▒labilir (AND); `fork` ile devral─▒n─▒r,
+    /// `exec` boyunca korunur (POSIX semanti─şi).
+    pub syscall_mask: u64,
+}
+
+impl Process {
+    pub const fn empty() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            pid: 0,
+            ppid: 0,
+            name: [0; 32],
+            name_len: 0,
+            state: ProcessState::Unused,
+            priority: ProcessPriority::Normal,
+            context: ProcessContext {
+                rax: 0,
+                rbx: 0,
+                rcx: 0,
+                rdx: 0,
+                rsi: 0,
+                rdi: 0,
+                rbp: 0,
+                rsp: 0,
+                r8: 0,
+                r9: 0,
+                r10: 0,
+                r11: 0,
+                r12: 0,
+                r13: 0,
+                r14: 0,
+                r15: 0,
+                rip: 0,
+                cs: 0,
+                rflags: 0x202,
+                ss: 0,
+                cr3: 0,
+                pcid: 0,
+                kernel_rsp: 0,
+            },
+            resources: ProcessResources::empty(),
+            address_space: AddressSpace {
+                pml4_phys: 0,
+                pcid: 0,
+            },
+            kstack_phys: 0,
+            kstack_top: 0,
+            resources_reaped: false,
+            cpu_time_ticks: 0,
+            exit_code: 0,
+            has_saved_user_context: false,
+            wait_event: None,
+            wait_deadline: None,
+            waiting_child: None,
+            wait_status_addr: 0,
+            heap_brk: 0,
+            mmap_base: 0,
+            mmap_used: 0,
+            mmap_regions: [crate::mm::user_mem::MmapRegion {
+                addr: 0,
+                len: 0,
+                used: false,
+            }; crate::mm::user_mem::MAX_MMAP_REGIONS],
+            fds: [FileDescriptor::empty(); crate::mm::fd::MAX_FDS],
+            pending_signals: 0,
+            pgid: 0,
+            sid: 0,
+            affinity: 0,
+            signal_handlers: [0; signals::MAX_SIGNAL as usize],
+            signal_mask: 0,
+            syscall_mask: u64::MAX,
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn name_str(&self) -> &str {
+        core::str::from_utf8(&self.name[..self.name_len]).unwrap_or("unknown")
+    }
+
+    /// `brk` heap'inde `offset` bayt─▒ndaki sanal adres.
+    pub fn heap_address(&self, offset: u64) -> u64 {
+        crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + offset
+    }
+
+    /// Heap ucu (son ge├ğerli bayttan bir sonraki adres).
+    pub fn heap_end(&self) -> u64 {
+        crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + self.heap_brk
+    }
+}
+
+/// Surec Tablosu ve Zamanlayici Yoneticisi
+pub struct ProcessManager {
+    pub processes: [Process; MAX_PROCESSES],
+    pub current_pid: usize,
+    pub next_pid: usize,
+    pub active_count: usize,
+    /// ├çekirdek genelinde a├ğ─▒k dosya nesneleri (fd i├ğerikleri, refcount).
+    pub files: FileTable,
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝re├ğleri i├ğin round-robin imleci (├ğekirdek slotu atlan─▒r).
+    user_rr: usize,
+    /// Sonraki kullan─▒lacak PCID de─şeri (kernel i├ğin 0 ayr─▒ld─▒).
+    /// TR / EN: Next PCID to assign (0 reserved for kernel).
+    next_pcid: u16,
+}
+
+/// S├╝rece ├Âzel ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ sayfa say─▒s─▒ (4 x 4 KiB = 16 KiB).
+pub const KSTACK_PAGES: usize = 4;
+
+impl ProcessManager {
+    pub const fn new() -> Self {
+        Self {
+            processes: [Process::empty(); MAX_PROCESSES],
+            current_pid: 0,
+            next_pid: 1,
+            active_count: 0,
+            files: FileTable::new(),
+            user_rr: 1,
+            next_pcid: 1, // 0 kernel i├ğin ayr─▒ld─▒
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Yeni bir PCID atar (kernel i├ğin 0 ayr─▒ld─▒). 4095'e ula┼ş─▒ld─▒─ş─▒nda
+    /// sarmalan─▒r (wraparound) ÔÇö pratikte s├╝re├ğ say─▒s─▒ s─▒n─▒rl─▒ oldu─şu i├ğin
+    /// ├ğak─▒┼şma riski d├╝┼ş├╝kt├╝r.
+    ///
+    /// INVPCID deste─şi olmayan i┼şlemcide PCID kullan─▒lmaz: t├╝m s├╝re├ğler
+    /// PCID=0 ile tek TLB ba─şlam─▒n─▒ payla┼ş─▒r (`invlpg` her zaman yeterli
+    /// olur). INVPCID varsa atanan PCID tek-ba─şlam invalidation ile
+    /// temizlenir ÔÇö PCID daha ├Ânce kullan─▒ld─▒ysa (sarmalama) eski sahibin
+    /// bayat ├ğevirileri yeni sahibin sayfa tablolar─▒yla ├ğak─▒┼şmas─▒n.
+    /// TR / EN: Assign a new PCID (0 reserved for kernel). Wraps at 4095.
+    fn alloc_pcid(&mut self) -> u16 {
+        if !crate::arch::x86_64::invpcid_supported() {
+            return 0;
+        }
+        let pcid = self.next_pcid;
+        crate::arch::x86_64::invpcid_flush_context(pcid);
+        self.next_pcid = if self.next_pcid >= 4095 {
+            1
+        } else {
+            self.next_pcid + 1
+        };
+        pcid
+    }
+
+    /// Initialize kernel process (PID 0 / init / kernel_core).
+    /// cekirdek baslangic surecini (PID 0 / init / kernel_core) kaydeder.
+    pub fn init_kernel_process(&mut self) {
+        let mut p = Process::empty();
+        p.pid = 0;
+        p.ppid = 0;
+        let name = b"kernel_core";
+        p.name[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
+        p.name_len = name.len();
+        p.state = ProcessState::Running;
+        p.priority = ProcessPriority::Realtime;
+        p.resources.limits = ResourceLimits::unlimited();
+        // Kernel sureci mevcut adres uzayini kullanir, PCID = 0
+        let mut aspace = AddressSpace::current();
+        aspace.pcid = 0;
+        p.address_space = aspace;
+        fd::init_standard_fds(&mut p.fds);
+        self.processes[0] = p;
+        self.current_pid = 0;
+        self.active_count = 1;
+    }
+
+    /// Spawn a new user or system application into the process table.
+    /// Creates a separate virtual address space (page table) and performs necessary mappings.
+    /// Yeni bir kullanici veya sistem uygulamasini surec tablosuna ekler (Spawn).
+    /// Ayr─▒ bir sanal adres uzay─▒ (page table) olu┼şturur ve gerekli e┼şlemeleri yapar.
+    pub fn spawn(
+        &mut self,
+        name: &str,
+        entry_point: u64,
+        stack_top: u64,
+        app_id: usize,
+        window_idx: Option<usize>,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> Result<usize, &'static str> {
+        let slot = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .position(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Unused)
+            .ok_or("Surec tablosu dolu: Yeni surec baslatilamadi")?;
+
+        let pid = self.next_pid;
+        self.next_pid = self.next_pid.saturating_add(1);
+
+        // Yeni bir PML4 k├Âk tablosu tahsis et (yeni adres uzay─▒ i├ğin)
+        let pml4_frame = pmm
+            .allocate_frame()
+            .ok_or("PMM yetersiz bellek: PML4 tablosu tahsis edilemedi")?;
+
+        // PML4'├╝ s─▒f─▒rla
+        unsafe {
+            let pml4_ptr = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(pml4_frame) as *mut u8;
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(pml4_ptr, 0, 4096);
+        }
+
+        let pcid = self.alloc_pcid();
+        let mut address_space = AddressSpace {
+            pml4_phys: pml4_frame,
+            pcid,
+        };
+
+        // Kernel mapping'leri yeni adres uzay─▒na kopyala (tam izolasyon i├ğin gerekli)
+        // Kernel e┼şlemesi kullan─▒c─▒ adres uzay─▒na kopyalanmal─▒ (tam izolasyon i├ğin).
+        if let Err(e) = address_space.clone_kernel_mappings(pmm) {
+            // Hata durumunda tahsis edilen frame'i iade et
+            pmm.free_frame(pml4_frame);
+            return Err(e);
+        }
+        // Payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ mmap arenas─▒n─▒n tablo yolunu ├Ânden kur: ilk
+        // `MAP_SHARED_FILE` e┼şlemesi PDPT/PD/PT tahsisi yapmas─▒n
+        // (best-effort; hata durumunda e┼şleme yine de tembel ├ğal─▒┼ş─▒r).
+        let _ = address_space.ensure_table_path(crate::mm::user_mem::SHARED_ARENA_BASE, pmm);
+
+        let mut p = Process::empty();
+        p.pid = pid;
+        p.ppid = self.current_pid;
+        let n = name.len().min(32);
+        p.name[..n].copy_from_slice(&name.as_bytes()[..n]);
+        p.name_len = n;
+        p.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+        p.priority = ProcessPriority::Normal;
+
+        // Baglami hazirla
+        p.context.rip = entry_point;
+        p.context.rsp = stack_top;
+        p.context.cr3 = pml4_frame; // Yeni CR3 de─şeri
+        p.context.pcid = pcid; // PCID
+        p.context.rflags = 0x202; // IF = 1 (Kesmeler acik)
+
+        p.resources.app_id = app_id;
+        p.resources.window_idx = window_idx;
+        p.address_space = address_space;
+        fd::init_standard_fds(&mut p.fds);
+        // POSIX: alt s├╝re├ğ ebeveynin grup/oturumunu devral─▒r. ├çekirdek
+        // s├╝recinin grubu yoksa (0) ├ğocuk kendi grubunu/oturumunu kurar.
+        let (ppid, parent_pgid, parent_sid, parent_affinity) = {
+            let parent = self.get_process(self.current_pid);
+            (
+                self.current_pid,
+                parent.map_or(0, |p| p.pgid),
+                parent.map_or(0, |p| p.sid),
+                parent.map_or(0, |p| p.affinity),
+            )
+        };
+        p.ppid = ppid;
+        p.pgid = if parent_pgid == 0 { pid } else { parent_pgid };
+        p.sid = if parent_sid == 0 { pid } else { parent_sid };
+        p.affinity = parent_affinity;
+        // ASLR: her s├╝re├ğ i├ğin mmap taban─▒ rastgele se├ğilir.
+        p.mmap_base = crate::mm::loader::randomized_mmap_base();
+
+        // Yeni PML4'├╝ kayna─şa ekle
+        if !p.resources.record_frame(pml4_frame) {
+            pmm.free_frame(pml4_frame);
+            return Err("Surec kaynak listesi dolu");
+        }
+
+        self.processes[slot] = p;
+        if !self.ensure_kernel_stack(pid, pmm) {
+            let process = &mut self.processes[slot];
+            process.state = ProcessState::Terminated;
+            Self::reap_process_resources(process, &mut self.files, pmm);
+            *process = Process::empty();
+            return Err("PMM yetersiz bellek: surec cekirdek yigini tahsis edilemedi");
+        }
+        self.active_count += 1;
+
+        Ok(pid)
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝recin kullan─▒c─▒ sayfa tablolar─▒n─▒ (PDPT/PD/PT) ve kay─▒tl─▒ t├╝m
+    /// fiziksel ├ğer├ğeveleri (PML4, ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒, kullan─▒c─▒ sayfalar─▒)
+    /// PMM'e iade eder. Yaln─▒zca s├╝recin CR3'├╝ art─▒k aktif de─şilken ├ğa─şr─▒lmal─▒.
+    fn reap_process_resources(
+        p: &mut Process,
+        files: &mut FileTable,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) {
+        if p.resources_reaped {
+            return;
+        }
+        // A├ğ─▒k dosya tan─▒t─▒c─▒lar─▒n─▒ kapat; son referans i├ğeri─şi serbest b─▒rak─▒r.
+        Self::close_fds(p, files);
+        // Heap/mmap e┼şlemelerini kald─▒r; fiziksel ├ğer├ğeveler a┼şa─ş─▒daki kaynak
+        // defteri ge├ği┼şinde tek seferde iade edilir (├ğift iade olmaz).
+        let _ = crate::mm::user_mem::release_user_memory(p, pmm);
+        // Sonra alt sayfa tablolar─▒: PML4 h├ól├ó ge├ğerliyken PDPT/PD/PT
+        // ├ğer├ğeveleri ├ğ├Âz├╝l├╝r. PML4 defterdedir, a┼şa─ş─▒daki d├Âng├╝de iade edilir.
+        // COW payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ ├ğer├ğeveler yaln─▒z defterden referans b─▒rak─▒r.
+        p.address_space.free_user_page_tables(pmm);
+        for &frame in p.resources.allocated_frames[..p.resources.frame_count].iter() {
+            if frame == 0 {
+                continue;
+            }
+            // Her ├ğer├ğeve i├ğin tek referans b─▒rak─▒l─▒r. ├çer├ğeve h├ól├ó ba┼şka bir
+            // adres uzay─▒ taraf─▒ndan payla┼ş─▒l─▒yorsa (`Some(false)`) fiziksel
+            // bellek serbest b─▒rak─▒lmaz.
+            let still_shared = matches!(crate::mm::vmm::release_frame_shared(frame), Some(false));
+            if !still_shared {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+        }
+        p.resources.frame_count = 0;
+        p.resources.user_frame_count = 0;
+        p.resources.allocated_frames = [0; MAX_PROC_FRAMES];
+        p.resources.user_allocated_frames = [0; MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES];
+        p.resources_reaped = true;
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝recin t├╝m kullan─▒c─▒ fd'lerini kapat─▒r, nesne referanslar─▒n─▒ b─▒rak─▒r ve
+    /// 0/1/2 standart tan─▒t─▒c─▒lar─▒n─▒ yeniden kurar.
+    fn close_fds(p: &mut Process, files: &mut FileTable) {
+        for object in fd::close_all_user_fds(&mut p.fds) {
+            files.release(object);
+        }
+        fd::init_standard_fds(&mut p.fds);
+    }
+
+    /// Terminate a process by PID and free its resources.
+    /// Belirtilen PID'ye sahip sureci sonlandirir (Kill/Terminate) ve kaynaklarini serbest birakir.
+    /// ├çal─▒┼şan s├╝recin kendisi i├ğin kaynak iadesi `reap_terminated`'e ertelenir;
+    /// kendi CR3'├╝ alt─▒ndaki sayfa tablolar─▒n─▒ yerinde imha etmemek i├ğin.
+    pub fn terminate(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        exit_code: i32,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 {
+            // cekirdek ana sureci sonlandirilamaz!
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        let is_current = pid == self.current_pid;
+        let Some(slot) = self.processes.iter().position(|p| {
+            p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+        }) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let parent_pid = self.processes[slot].ppid;
+        self.processes[slot].state = ProcessState::Terminated;
+        self.processes[slot].exit_code = exit_code;
+        self.active_count = self.active_count.saturating_sub(1);
+
+        // 1. Kullan─▒c─▒ alt sayfa tablolar─▒n─▒ serbest b─▒rak (PML4 klonlar─▒ hari├ğ)
+        // 2. S├╝recin sahip oldu─şu t├╝m fiziksel sayfa ├ğer├ğevelerini PMM'e iade et
+        //    (PML4, ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ ve kay─▒tl─▒ kullan─▒c─▒ sayfalar─▒ dahil)
+        if !is_current {
+            Self::reap_process_resources(&mut self.processes[slot], &mut self.files, pmm);
+        }
+        self.wake_waiting_parent(pid, parent_pid, exit_code);
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝reci sonland─▒r─▒lm─▒┼ş olarak i┼şaretler; kaynak iadesi `reap_terminated`
+    /// ├ğa─şr─▒s─▒na ertelenir. `int 0x80` (SYS_EXIT) gibi s├╝recin kendi CR3'├╝
+    /// alt─▒nda ├ğal─▒┼şan ba─şlamlardan g├╝venle ├ğa─şr─▒labilir.
+    pub fn mark_exited(&mut self, pid: usize, exit_code: i32) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let Some(slot) = self.processes.iter().position(|p| {
+            p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+        }) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let parent_pid = self.processes[slot].ppid;
+        self.processes[slot].state = ProcessState::Terminated;
+        self.processes[slot].exit_code = exit_code;
+        self.active_count = self.active_count.saturating_sub(1);
+        // ├ç─▒k─▒┼şta fd'ler hemen kapan─▒r; bellek iadesi reaping'e ertelenir.
+        Self::close_fds(&mut self.processes[slot], &mut self.files);
+        for child in self.processes.iter_mut() {
+            if child.ppid == pid && child.state != ProcessState::Unused {
+                child.ppid = 0;
+            }
+        }
+        self.wake_waiting_parent(pid, parent_pid, exit_code);
+        true
+    }
+
+    fn wake_waiting_parent(&mut self, pid: usize, parent_pid: usize, exit_code: i32) {
+        let Some(slot) = self.processes.iter().position(|parent| {
+            parent.pid == parent_pid
+                && parent.state == ProcessState::Blocked
+                && parent
+                    .waiting_child
+                    .is_some_and(|waiting| waiting == 0 || waiting == pid)
+        }) else {
+            return;
+        };
+        let parent = &mut self.processes[slot];
+        parent.context.rax = pid as u64;
+        if parent.wait_status_addr != 0 {
+            Self::write_wait_status(parent, parent.wait_status_addr, exit_code);
+        }
+        parent.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+        parent.waiting_child = None;
+        parent.wait_status_addr = 0;
+    }
+
+    fn write_wait_status(parent: &Process, address: u64, exit_code: i32) -> bool {
+        uaccess::copy_to_user(&parent.address_space, address, &exit_code.to_le_bytes()).is_ok()
+    }
+
+    /// `Terminated` durumundaki ve kaynaklar─▒ hen├╝z iade edilmemi┼ş s├╝re├ğlerin
+    /// sayfa ├ğer├ğevelerini, alt sayfa tablolar─▒n─▒ ve ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒nlar─▒n─▒ salar.
+    /// Yaln─▒zca ├ğekirdek CR3'├╝ aktifken (kullan─▒c─▒ modunda de─şilken) ├ğa─şr─▒lmal─▒.
+    pub fn reap_terminated(&mut self, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> usize {
+        let mut reaped = 0usize;
+        for p in self.processes.iter_mut() {
+            if p.state == ProcessState::Terminated && !p.resources_reaped {
+                Self::reap_process_resources(p, &mut self.files, pmm);
+                reaped += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        reaped
+    }
+
+    /// Find and terminate a running process by window index.
+    /// Pencere indeksine gore calisan sureci bulur ve sonlandirir.
+    pub fn terminate_by_window(&mut self, win_idx: usize, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+        let target_pid = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .find(|p| {
+                p.state != ProcessState::Unused
+                    && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+                    && p.resources.window_idx == Some(win_idx)
+            })
+            .map(|p| p.pid);
+
+        if let Some(pid) = target_pid {
+            self.terminate(pid, 0, pmm)
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Mevcut ├ğal─▒┼şan s├╝reci fault sonras─▒ temizler (Ring 3 fault cleanup).
+    /// S├╝recin kaynaklar─▒n─▒ serbest b─▒rak─▒r ve durumunu Terminated olarak i┼şaretler.
+    pub fn cleanup_faulted_process(&mut self, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+        let current = self.current_pid;
+        if current == 0 {
+            // Kernel s├╝reci temizlenemez
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if let Some(p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == current) {
+            p.state = ProcessState::Terminated;
+            p.exit_code = -11; // SIGSEGV
+            self.active_count = self.active_count.saturating_sub(1);
+            Self::reap_process_resources(p, &mut self.files, pmm);
+            true
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝rece ├Âzel ├ğekirdek y─▒─ş─▒n─▒n─▒ (Ring3ÔåÆRing0 ge├ği┼ş y─▒─ş─▒n─▒) tembel olarak
+    /// tahsis eder. 4 fiziksel sayfa (16 KiB) contiguous ayr─▒l─▒r, HHDM sanal
+    /// adresi `kstack_top` olarak kaydedilir ve ├ğer├ğeveler kaynak listesine
+    /// i┼şlenir (b├Âylece `reap_process_resources` onlar─▒ da iade eder).
+    pub fn ensure_kernel_stack(&mut self, pid: usize, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+        let Some(p) = self
+            .processes
+            .iter_mut()
+            .find(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused)
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        if p.kstack_top != 0 {
+            return true;
+        }
+        let Some(base) = pmm.allocate_contiguous_frames(KSTACK_PAGES) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        // Y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ s─▒f─▒rla; ard─▒ndan tepesini 16 bayt hizas─▒na indir.
+        unsafe {
+            let vbase = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(base) as *mut u8;
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(vbase, 0, (KSTACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE) as usize);
+        }
+        let top_virt = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(base + KSTACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE);
+        // ├çer├ğeveleri kaynak listesine i┼şle; biri bile s─▒─şmazsa geri al.
+        let mut recorded = 0usize;
+        for i in 0..KSTACK_PAGES {
+            let frame = base + i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            if !p.resources.record_frame(frame) {
+                break;
+            }
+            recorded += 1;
+        }
+        if recorded != KSTACK_PAGES {
+            for i in 0..recorded {
+                let frame = base + i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+                p.resources.take_frame(frame);
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+            return false;
+        }
+        p.kstack_phys = base;
+        p.kstack_top = top_virt & !0xF;
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// ├çocuk adres uzay─▒n─▒ copy-on-write semanti─şiyle kurar: ebeveynin
+    /// yaz─▒labilir kullan─▒c─▒ sayfalar─▒ iki adres uzay─▒nda da salt-okunur
+    /// yap─▒l─▒p payla┼ş─▒l─▒r (`PAGE_COW`), ilk yazmada `handle_cow_fault`
+    /// sayfay─▒ ├Âzel kopyaya d├Ân├╝┼şt├╝r├╝r. Yaln─▒zca yaz─▒labilirlik biti hi├ğ
+    /// ta┼ş─▒mam─▒┼ş (salt-okunur kod, RO veri) sayfalar do─şrudan payla┼ş─▒l─▒r.
+    ///
+    /// `parent_frames` / `child_frames` ilk hata durumunda geri al─▒nacak
+    /// "bu fork s─▒ras─▒nda payla┼ş─▒ma a├ğ─▒lan" ├ğer├ğeveleri ta┼ş─▒r.
+    unsafe fn cow_clone_user_table(
+        table_phys: u64,
+        level: u8,
+        prefix: u64,
+        parent: &mut Process,
+        child: &mut Process,
+        parent_frames: &mut alloc::vec::Vec<u64>,
+        child_frames: &mut alloc::vec::Vec<u64>,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
+        let entries = (&*AddressSpace::table_ptr(table_phys)).entries;
+        let shift = 12 + 9 * (level as u32 - 1);
+        for (index, entry) in entries.into_iter().enumerate() {
+            if entry & (PageFlags::PRESENT | PageFlags::USER)
+                != PageFlags::PRESENT | PageFlags::USER
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let virtual_address = prefix | ((index as u64) << shift);
+            if level == 1 {
+                if entry & PAGE_LAZY != 0 {
+                    // Tembel sayfa: fiziksel ├ğer├ğeve yok. ├çocuk ayn─▒ tembel PTE'yi
+                    // al─▒r; her iki taraf da ilk yazmada kendi ├ğer├ğevesini ay─▒r─▒r
+                    // (COW payla┼ş─▒m─▒ ve kota t├╝ketimi yok).
+                    if child
+                        .address_space
+                        .map_page(virtual_address, 0, entry, pmm)
+                        .is_err()
+                    {
+                        return Err("Fork tembel sayfasi eslenemedi");
+                    }
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let frame = entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000;
+                let writable = entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE != 0;
+                let mut child_flags = entry & !0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000;
+                if writable && !crate::mm::vmm::mark_frame_shared(frame) {
+                    // COW defteri dolu: ├ğer├ğeveyi ├Âzel kopya olarak ay─▒r.
+                    let copy = pmm
+                        .allocate_frame()
+                        .ok_or("PMM yetersiz bellek: fork sayfasi tahsis edilemedi")?;
+                    let source = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(frame) as *const u8;
+                    let destination = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(copy) as *mut u8;
+                    core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(source, destination, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
+                    if !child.resources.record_frame(copy) {
+                        pmm.free_frame(copy);
+                        return Err("Surec kaynak listesi dolu: fork");
+                    }
+                    if child
+                        .address_space
+                        .map_page(virtual_address, copy, child_flags, pmm)
+                        .is_err()
+                    {
+                        child.resources.take_frame(copy);
+                        pmm.free_frame(copy);
+                        return Err("Fork sayfasi eslenemedi");
+                    }
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if writable {
+                    // Payla┼ş─▒m kuruldu: iki taraf da salt-okunur + COW.
+                    child_flags &= !PageFlags::WRITABLE;
+                    child_flags |= PAGE_COW;
+                    if parent
+                        .address_space
+                        .set_flags(virtual_address, child_flags, pmm)
+                        .is_err()
+                    {
+                        // Ebeveyn sayfas─▒ COW yap─▒lamad─▒: saya├ğ kayd─▒n─▒ geri al.
+                        let _ = crate::mm::vmm::release_frame_shared(frame);
+                        return Err("Fork: ebeveyn sayfasi COW yapilamadi");
+                    }
+                    parent_frames.push(frame);
+                    child_frames.push(frame);
+                }
+                if child
+                    .address_space
+                    .map_page(virtual_address, frame, child_flags, pmm)
+                    .is_err()
+                {
+                    return Err("Fork sayfasi eslenemedi");
+                }
+            } else {
+                if entry & PageFlags::HUGE_PAGE != 0 {
+                    return Err("Fork buyuk kullanici sayfalarini desteklemiyor");
+                }
+                Self::cow_clone_user_table(
+                    entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000,
+                    level - 1,
+                    virtual_address,
+                    parent,
+                    child,
+                    parent_frames,
+                    child_frames,
+                    pmm,
+                )?;
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    /// COW sayfalar─▒n─▒ eski haline getirir (fork geri al─▒n─▒rken).
+    fn rollback_cow_shares(
+        parent: &mut Process,
+        child: &mut Process,
+        shared: &[u64],
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) {
+        for &frame in shared {
+            let _ = crate::mm::vmm::release_frame_shared(frame);
+        }
+        // Ebeveynin yaz─▒labilirli─şini geri y├╝kle.
+        let pml4 = parent.address_space.pml4_phys;
+        let child_pml4 = child.address_space.pml4_phys;
+        if pml4 == 0 || child_pml4 == 0 {
+            return;
+        }
+        // Yaln─▒zca h├ól├ó COW bayrakl─▒ (yani bu fork'ta payla┼ş─▒lan ve hen├╝z
+        // yazma ile ├Âzel kopyaya ayr─▒lmam─▒┼ş) sayfalar geri al─▒n─▒r.
+        unsafe {
+            let source = &*AddressSpace::table_ptr(child_pml4);
+            for (index, pdpt_entry) in source.entries.iter().enumerate().take(256) {
+                if pdpt_entry & PageFlags::PRESENT == 0 || pdpt_entry & PageFlags::HUGE_PAGE != 0 {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let pdpt = &*AddressSpace::table_ptr(pdpt_entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000);
+                for (pdpt_idx, pd_entry) in pdpt.entries.iter().enumerate() {
+                    if pd_entry & PageFlags::PRESENT == 0 || pd_entry & PageFlags::HUGE_PAGE != 0 {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    let pd = &*AddressSpace::table_ptr(pd_entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000);
+                    for (pd_idx, pt_entry) in pd.entries.iter().enumerate() {
+                        if pt_entry & PageFlags::PRESENT == 0
+                            || pt_entry & PageFlags::HUGE_PAGE != 0
+                        {
+                            continue;
+                        }
+                        let pt = &*AddressSpace::table_ptr(pt_entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000);
+                        for (pt_idx, leaf) in pt.entries.iter().enumerate() {
+                            if leaf & (PageFlags::PRESENT | PAGE_COW)
+                                != PageFlags::PRESENT | PAGE_COW
+                            {
+                                continue;
+                            }
+                            let address = ((index as u64) << 39)
+                                | ((pdpt_idx as u64) << 30)
+                                | ((pd_idx as u64) << 21)
+                                | ((pt_idx as u64) << 12);
+                            let restored = (leaf & !PAGE_COW) | PageFlags::WRITABLE;
+                            let _ = parent.address_space.set_flags(address, restored, pmm);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Creates a child whose user mappings are shared copy-on-write with the
+    /// parent. Frames become private copies on the first write (`handle_cow_fault`).
+    pub fn fork_process(
+        &mut self,
+        parent_pid: usize,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> Result<usize, &'static str> {
+        let parent = self
+            .get_process(parent_pid)
+            .copied()
+            .filter(|process| process.state != ProcessState::Terminated)
+            .ok_or("Fork ana sureci bulunamadi")?;
+        let slot = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .position(|process| process.state == ProcessState::Unused)
+            .ok_or("Surec tablosu dolu: Fork yapilamadi")?;
+        let root = pmm
+            .allocate_frame()
+            .ok_or("PMM yetersiz bellek: fork PML4 tahsis edilemedi")?;
+        unsafe {
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(
+                crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(root) as *mut u8,
+                0,
+                PAGE_SIZE as usize,
+            );
+        }
+        let child_pcid = self.alloc_pcid();
+        let mut child = Process::empty();
+        child.pid = self.next_pid;
+        self.next_pid = self.next_pid.saturating_add(1);
+        child.ppid = parent_pid;
+        child.name = parent.name;
+        child.name_len = parent.name_len;
+        child.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+        child.priority = parent.priority;
+        child.context = parent.context;
+        child.context.cr3 = root;
+        child.context.pcid = child_pcid;
+        child.context.rax = 0;
+        child.context.kernel_rsp = 0;
+        child.has_saved_user_context = parent.has_saved_user_context;
+        child.resources.app_id = parent.resources.app_id;
+        child.resources.window_idx = parent.resources.window_idx;
+        // Kaynak limitleri fork ile devral─▒n─▒r; ├ğer├ğeve defteri bu limitlerle
+        // doldurulmadan ├Ânce kopyalanmal─▒d─▒r.
+        child.resources.limits = parent.resources.limits;
+        // Heap ve mmap durumu da miras al─▒n─▒r (fork semanti─şi).
+        child.heap_brk = parent.heap_brk;
+        child.mmap_base = parent.mmap_base;
+        child.mmap_used = parent.mmap_used;
+        child.mmap_regions = parent.mmap_regions;
+        // Fork, a├ğ─▒k fd tablosunu devral─▒r (POSIX dup semanti─şi); nesne
+        // referanslar─▒ yaln─▒zca fork ba┼şar─▒yla tamamlan─▒nca art─▒r─▒l─▒r.
+        child.fds = parent.fds;
+        // Grup/oturum, CPU yak─▒nl─▒─ş─▒ ve sinyal da─ş─▒t─▒m─▒ fork ile devral─▒n─▒r.
+        child.pgid = parent.pgid;
+        child.sid = parent.sid;
+        child.affinity = parent.affinity;
+        child.signal_handlers = parent.signal_handlers;
+        child.signal_mask = parent.signal_mask;
+        child.syscall_mask = parent.syscall_mask;
+        // context.pcid ile address_space.pcid ayn─▒ de─şeri ta┼ş─▒mal─▒d─▒r;
+        // iki ayr─▒ alloc_pcid ├ğa─şr─▒s─▒ PCID sarmalamas─▒n─▒ iki kat h─▒zland─▒r─▒r
+        // ve restore()'un yazd─▒─ş─▒ PCID ile adres uzay─▒n─▒n kayd─▒ ├ğeli┼şirdi.
+        child.address_space = AddressSpace {
+            pml4_phys: root,
+            pcid: child_pcid,
+        };
+        child.resources.record_frame(root);
+
+        if let Err(error) = child.address_space.clone_kernel_mappings(pmm) {
+            pmm.free_frame(root);
+            return Err(error);
+        }
+        // Payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ mmap arenas─▒n─▒n tablo yolunu ├Ânden kur (spawn ile ayn─▒
+        // gerek├ğe; best-effort).
+        let _ = child
+            .address_space
+            .ensure_table_path(crate::mm::user_mem::SHARED_ARENA_BASE, pmm);
+        let mut parent_process = parent;
+        let mut cow_frames: alloc::vec::Vec<u64> = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+        // Not: a┼şa─ş─▒daki `cow_clone_user_table` ├ğa─şr─▒s─▒ ebeveynin PTE'lerini
+        // salt-okunur + PAGE_COW yapar; bu y├╝zden tablo giri┼şleri
+        // `parent_process.address_space` ├╝zerinden okunur.
+        let clone_result = unsafe {
+            let source = parent_process.address_space.pml4_phys;
+            let pml4 = &*AddressSpace::table_ptr(source);
+            let mut result = Ok(());
+            for (index, entry) in pml4.entries.iter().enumerate().take(256) {
+                if entry & (PageFlags::PRESENT | PageFlags::USER)
+                    == PageFlags::PRESENT | PageFlags::USER
+                {
+                    let prefix = (index as u64) << 39;
+                    let mut child_frames: alloc::vec::Vec<u64> = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+                    if let Err(error) = Self::cow_clone_user_table(
+                        entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000,
+                        3,
+                        prefix,
+                        &mut parent_process,
+                        &mut child,
+                        &mut cow_frames,
+                        &mut child_frames,
+                        pmm,
+                    ) {
+                        result = Err(error);
+                        break;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            result
+        };
+        if let Err(error) = clone_result {
+            Self::rollback_cow_shares(&mut parent_process, &mut child, &cow_frames, pmm);
+            child.address_space.free_user_page_tables(pmm);
+            for &frame in child
+                .resources
+                .allocated_frames
+                .iter()
+                .take(child.resources.frame_count)
+            {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+            return Err(error);
+        }
+        // Payla┼ş─▒lan ├ğer├ğeveleri ├ğocuk kaynak listesine i┼şle; b├Âylece ├ğocuk
+        // ├ğ─▒k─▒┼ş─▒nda referans saya├ğlar─▒ do─şru b─▒rak─▒l─▒r.
+        for &frame in &cow_frames {
+            let _ = child.resources.record_frame(frame);
+        }
+        if !Self::allocate_kernel_stack_for(&mut child, pmm) {
+            Self::rollback_cow_shares(&mut parent_process, &mut child, &cow_frames, pmm);
+            child.address_space.free_user_page_tables(pmm);
+            for &frame in child
+                .resources
+                .allocated_frames
+                .iter()
+                .take(child.resources.frame_count)
+            {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+            return Err("PMM yetersiz bellek: fork cekirdek yigini tahsis edilemedi");
+        }
+
+        let child_pid = child.pid;
+        // Devral─▒nan fd'ler i├ğin nesne referanslar─▒n─▒ art─▒r.
+        for entry in child.fds.iter() {
+            if entry.used && entry.kind == FileKind::Regular {
+                if let Some(object) = self.files.objects.get_mut(entry.object as usize) {
+                    if object.used {
+                        object.refs = object.refs.saturating_add(1);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        self.processes[slot] = child;
+        self.active_count += 1;
+        // Ebeveynin ├ğer├ğeve bayraklar─▒ (COW demote) g├╝ncellendi; s├╝re├ğ
+        // kayd─▒n─▒ geri yaz.
+        if let Some(parent) = self.get_process_mut(parent_pid) {
+            *parent = parent_process;
+            parent.context.rax = child_pid as u64;
+        }
+        Ok(child_pid)
+    }
+
+    /// Copy-on-write sayfa hatas─▒n─▒ i┼şler. `fault_addr` i├ğeren 4KB sayfa
+    /// `PAGE_COW` i┼şaretliyse ├ğer├ğeve ├Âzel kopyaya d├Ân├╝┼şt├╝r├╝l├╝r ve TLB
+    /// ge├ğersizle┼ştirilir; ├ğa─ş─▒ran kesme ├ğer├ğevesini yeniden denemek ├╝zere
+    /// b─▒rak─▒r. Sayfa COW de─şilse `false` d├Âner (ger├ğek hata).
+    ///
+    /// Ba┼şka bir adres uzay─▒ndaki ├ğer├ğeve HHDM ├╝zerinden kopyalan─▒r; bu
+    /// y├╝zden yaln─▒zca ├ğekirdek CR3'├╝ aktifken de g├╝venlidir.
+    pub fn handle_cow_fault(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        fault_addr: u64,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> bool {
+        let page = fault_addr & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
+        let Some(slot) = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .position(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated)
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let entry = match self.processes[slot].address_space.read_entry(page) {
+            Some(entry) => entry,
+            None => return false,
+        };
+        // Tembel sayfa COW de─şildir; ├ğer├ğevesi yoktur.
+        if entry & PAGE_LAZY != 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if entry & PAGE_COW == 0 || entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE != 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let shared_frame = entry & 0x000F_FFFF_FFFF_F000;
+        let private_flags = (entry & !PAGE_COW) | PageFlags::WRITABLE;
+
+        // Payla┼ş─▒m tek eldeyse kopya gerekmez: ├ğer├ğeveyi yerinde yaz─▒labilir yap.
+        let release = crate::mm::vmm::release_frame_shared(shared_frame);
+        let copy_needed = !matches!(release, Some(true));
+        // GE├ç─░C─░ TE┼ŞH─░S: COW yol se├ğimini ve payla┼ş─▒m sayac─▒n─▒ izle.
+        crate::serial_print("[COW-DBG] pid=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(pid as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" page=");
+        crate::serial::print_hex(page);
+        crate::serial_print(" frame=");
+        crate::serial::print_hex(shared_frame);
+        crate::serial_print(" release=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(match release {
+            Some(true) => 1,
+            Some(false) => 2,
+            None => 3,
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(" copy_needed=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(copy_needed as u64);
+        crate::serial_print("\n");
+        if !copy_needed {
+            return self.processes[slot]
+                .address_space
+                .set_flags(page, private_flags, pmm)
+                .is_ok();
+        }
+
+        let Some(copy) = pmm.allocate_frame() else {
+            // Bellek yok: sayfay─▒ payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ (salt-okunur) b─▒rak, yazma tekrar hata verir.
+            let _ = crate::mm::vmm::mark_frame_shared(shared_frame);
+            return false;
+        };
+        unsafe {
+            let source = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(shared_frame) as *const u8;
+            let destination = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(copy) as *mut u8;
+            core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(source, destination, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
+        }
+        // GE├ç─░C─░ TE┼ŞH─░S: ayr─▒lan ├Âzel kopya ├ğer├ğevesini izle.
+        crate::serial_print("[COW-DBG] copy=");
+        crate::serial::print_hex(copy);
+        crate::serial_print("\n");
+        if !self.processes[slot].resources.record_user_frame(copy) {
+            pmm.free_frame(copy);
+            let _ = crate::mm::vmm::mark_frame_shared(shared_frame);
+            return false;
+        }
+        if self.processes[slot]
+            .address_space
+            .remap_page(page, copy, pmm)
+            .is_err()
+        {
+            self.processes[slot].resources.take_user_frame(copy);
+            pmm.free_frame(copy);
+            let _ = crate::mm::vmm::mark_frame_shared(shared_frame);
+            return false;
+        }
+        let resources = &mut self.processes[slot].resources;
+        if !resources.take_user_frame(shared_frame) && !resources.take_frame(shared_frame) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        // Yeni ├ğer├ğeve ├Âzel; payla┼ş─▒m kayd─▒ndan ├ğ─▒kart─▒ld─▒.
+        self.processes[slot]
+            .address_space
+            .set_flags(page, private_flags, pmm)
+            .is_ok()
+    }
+
+    /// `[addr, addr+len)` aral─▒─ş─▒ndaki tembel kullan─▒c─▒ sayfalar─▒n─▒
+    /// ger├ğekle┼ştirir. Syscall ├ğ─▒kt─▒ tamponlar─▒ (MEMINFO/RESINFO/READ)
+    /// hen├╝z yaz─▒lmam─▒┼ş lazy sayfalara i┼şaret edebilir; salt-okunur s─▒f─▒r
+    /// sayfas─▒ `uaccess` yazmas─▒n─▒ reddetti─şinden kopyadan ├Ânce ├ğa─şr─▒l─▒r.
+    /// Tembel olmayan sayfalar `handle_lazy_fault` taraf─▒ndan yok say─▒l─▒r.
+    pub fn realize_lazy_range(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        addr: u64,
+        len: u64,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) {
+        if addr == 0 || len == 0 {
+            return;
+        }
+        let mut offset = 0u64;
+        while offset < len {
+            let page_addr = (addr + offset) & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
+            let _ = self.handle_lazy_fault(pid, page_addr, pmm);
+            offset += PAGE_SIZE;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Tembel (demand) sayfa hatas─▒n─▒ i┼şler: `PAGE_LAZY` i┼şaretli kullan─▒c─▒
+    /// sayfas─▒ i├ğin s─▒f─▒rlanm─▒┼ş ├ğer├ğeve ay─▒r─▒r, kaynak defterine i┼şler ve
+    /// PTE'yi kal─▒c─▒ bayraklar (`USER_DATA`) ile de─şi┼ştirir. Ba┼şar─▒da `true`
+    /// d├Âner ve ├ğa─ş─▒ran komutu yeniden ├ğal─▒┼şt─▒r─▒r.
+    pub fn handle_lazy_fault(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        fault_addr: u64,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> bool {
+        let page = fault_addr & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
+        let Some(slot) = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .position(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated)
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let Some(entry) = self.processes[slot].address_space.read_entry(page) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        if entry & PAGE_LAZY == 0 || entry & PageFlags::USER == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let Some(frame) = pmm.allocate_frame() else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        // S─▒f─▒rlanm─▒┼ş sayfa: anonim bellek s├Âzle┼şmesi.
+        unsafe {
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(
+                crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(frame) as *mut u8,
+                0,
+                PAGE_SIZE as usize,
+            );
+        }
+        if !self.processes[slot].resources.record_user_frame(frame) {
+            pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            return false;
+        }
+        let flags = PageFlags::USER_DATA;
+        if self.processes[slot]
+            .address_space
+            .map_page(page, frame, flags, pmm)
+            .is_err()
+        {
+            self.processes[slot].resources.take_user_frame(frame);
+            pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            return false;
+        }
+        LAZY_FAULTS.fetch_add(1, core::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝re├ğ adres uzay─▒nda tembel sayfa say─▒s─▒ (heap + mmap).
+    pub fn lazy_pages(&self, pid: usize) -> usize {
+        let Some(process) = self.get_process(pid) else {
+            return 0;
+        };
+        let mut total = crate::mm::user_mem::lazy_page_count(
+            process,
+            crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE,
+            crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + process.heap_brk,
+        );
+        for region in process.mmap_regions.iter().filter(|region| region.used) {
+            total += crate::mm::user_mem::lazy_page_count(
+                process,
+                region.addr,
+                region.addr + region.len,
+            );
+        }
+        total
+    }
+
+    fn allocate_kernel_stack_for(p: &mut Process, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+        if p.kstack_top != 0 {
+            return true;
+        }
+        let Some(base) = pmm.allocate_contiguous_frames(KSTACK_PAGES) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        unsafe {
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(
+                crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(base) as *mut u8,
+                0,
+                (KSTACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE) as usize,
+            );
+        }
+        for i in 0..KSTACK_PAGES {
+            let frame = base + i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            if !p.resources.record_frame(frame) {
+                for allocated in 0..i {
+                    let owned = base + allocated as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+                    p.resources.take_frame(owned);
+                    pmm.free_frame(owned);
+                }
+                pmm.free_contiguous_frames(base + i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE, KSTACK_PAGES - i);
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+        p.kstack_phys = base;
+        p.kstack_top = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(base + KSTACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE) & !0xF;
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// Executes a new raw or ELF image in a fresh address space, preserving only
+    /// the process's kernel stack. A failed load leaves the old image intact.
+    pub fn exec_process(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        image: &[u8],
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
+        let old = self
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .copied()
+            .filter(|process| process.state != ProcessState::Terminated)
+            .ok_or("Exec sureci bulunamadi")?;
+        let root = pmm
+            .allocate_frame()
+            .ok_or("PMM yetersiz bellek: exec PML4 tahsis edilemedi")?;
+        unsafe {
+            core::ptr::write_bytes(
+                crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(root) as *mut u8,
+                0,
+                PAGE_SIZE as usize,
+            );
+        }
+        let exec_pcid = self.alloc_pcid();
+        let mut new_space = AddressSpace {
+            pml4_phys: root,
+            pcid: exec_pcid,
+        };
+        if let Err(error) = new_space.clone_kernel_mappings(pmm) {
+            pmm.free_frame(root);
+            return Err(error);
+        }
+        // Payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ mmap arenas─▒n─▒n tablo yolunu ├Ânden kur (spawn ile ayn─▒
+        // gerek├ğe; best-effort).
+        let _ = new_space.ensure_table_path(crate::mm::user_mem::SHARED_ARENA_BASE, pmm);
+        let loaded = match crate::mm::loader::AppLoader::load_binary(image, &mut new_space, pmm) {
+            Ok(loaded) if loaded.allocated_pages <= crate::mm::loader::MAX_LOADED_FRAMES => loaded,
+            Ok(_) => {
+                new_space.destroy_user_address_space(pmm);
+                return Err("Exec imaji kaynak sinirini asti");
+            }
+            Err(error) => {
+                new_space.destroy_user_address_space(pmm);
+                return Err(error);
+            }
+        };
+
+        let mut new_resources = ProcessResources::empty();
+        // Kaynak limitleri exec boyunca korunur (yaln─▒zca fd tablosu s─▒f─▒rlan─▒r).
+        new_resources.limits = old.resources.limits;
+        new_resources.app_id = old.resources.app_id;
+        new_resources.window_idx = old.resources.window_idx;
+        if !new_resources.record_frame(root) {
+            new_space.destroy_user_address_space(pmm);
+            return Err("Exec kaynak listesi dolu");
+        }
+        let stack_frame_count = if old.kstack_phys == 0 {
+            0
+        } else {
+            KSTACK_PAGES
+        };
+        for i in 0..stack_frame_count {
+            let frame = old.kstack_phys + i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            if !new_resources.record_frame(frame) {
+                new_space.destroy_user_address_space(pmm);
+                return Err("Exec cekirdek yigini kaynak listesine sigmadi");
+            }
+        }
+        for &frame in loaded.allocated_frames.iter().take(loaded.allocated_pages) {
+            if !new_resources.record_frame(frame) {
+                new_space.destroy_user_address_space(pmm);
+                return Err("Exec kaynak listesi dolu");
+            }
+        }
+
+        let was_active = AddressSpace::current().pml4_phys == old.address_space.pml4_phys;
+        let old_resources = old.resources;
+        let mut old_space = old.address_space;
+        // Ba┼şar─▒l─▒ exec: t├╝m kullan─▒c─▒ fd'leri kapan─▒r (CLOEXEC davran─▒┼ş─▒) ve
+        // 0/1/2 standart tan─▒t─▒c─▒lar─▒ yeniden kurulur.
+        if let Some(slot) = self.processes.iter().position(|p| p.pid == pid) {
+            Self::close_fds(&mut self.processes[slot], &mut self.files);
+        }
+        if let Some(process) = self.get_process_mut(pid) {
+            process.address_space = new_space;
+            process.resources = new_resources;
+            process.resources_reaped = false;
+            process.context.rip = loaded.entry_point;
+            process.context.rsp = loaded.stack_pointer;
+            process.context.cr3 = root;
+            process.context.pcid = exec_pcid;
+            process.context.kernel_rsp = 0;
+            process.has_saved_user_context = false;
+            // Yeni imaj: heap/mm b├Âlgeleri s─▒f─▒rlan─▒r, mmap taban─▒ yeniden
+            // rastgelele┼ştirilir (exec sonras─▒ ASLR).
+            process.heap_brk = 0;
+            process.mmap_used = 0;
+            process.mmap_regions = Process::empty().mmap_regions;
+            process.mmap_base = crate::mm::loader::randomized_mmap_base();
+            // exec sonras─▒ bekleyen sinyaller ve kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicileri
+            // s─▒f─▒rlan─▒r; grup/oturum, affinity ve sinyal maskesi korunur
+            // (POSIX exec semanti─şi).
+            process.pending_signals = 0;
+            process.signal_handlers = [0; signals::MAX_SIGNAL as usize];
+        }
+        if was_active {
+            unsafe {
+                let new_aspace = AddressSpace {
+                    pml4_phys: root,
+                    pcid: exec_pcid,
+                };
+                new_aspace.load_cr3();
+            }
+        }
+        old_space.free_user_page_tables(pmm);
+        for &frame in old_resources
+            .allocated_frames
+            .iter()
+            .take(old_resources.frame_count)
+        {
+            let is_kernel_stack = old.kstack_phys != 0
+                && frame >= old.kstack_phys
+                && frame < old.kstack_phys + KSTACK_PAGES as u64 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            if frame != 0
+                && !is_kernel_stack
+                && !matches!(crate::mm::vmm::release_frame_shared(frame), Some(false))
+            {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    /// Reaps one terminated child, or reports that a live child is still running.
+    pub fn wait_child(
+        &mut self,
+        parent_pid: usize,
+        child_pid: Option<usize>,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> Result<Option<(usize, i32)>, &'static str> {
+        let matching_child = self.processes.iter().position(|child| {
+            child.state != ProcessState::Unused
+                && child.pid != 0
+                && child.ppid == parent_pid
+                && child_pid.is_none_or(|pid| child.pid == pid)
+        });
+        let Some(slot) = matching_child else {
+            return Err("Beklenen alt surec bulunamadi");
+        };
+        if self.processes[slot].state != ProcessState::Terminated {
+            return Ok(None);
+        }
+        let pid = self.processes[slot].pid;
+        let exit_code = self.processes[slot].exit_code;
+        Self::reap_process_resources(&mut self.processes[slot], &mut self.files, pmm);
+        self.processes[slot] = Process::empty();
+        Ok(Some((pid, exit_code)))
+    }
+
+    /// PID ile de─şi┼ştirilebilir s├╝re├ğ referans─▒.
+    pub fn get_process_mut(&mut self, pid: usize) -> Option<&mut Process> {
+        self.processes
+            .iter_mut()
+            .find(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused)
+    }
+
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝re├ğleri zamanlay─▒c─▒s─▒ i├ğin: `Ready` durumunda ve ger├ğek
+    /// bir kullan─▒c─▒ giri┼ş noktas─▒ olan (rip/rsp tan─▒ml─▒) ilk s├╝reci
+    /// round-robin s─▒ras─▒yla d├Ând├╝r├╝r. GUI s├Âzde-s├╝re├ğleri (rip=0) atlan─▒r.
+    pub fn next_ready_user_process(&mut self) -> Option<usize> {
+        for _ in 0..MAX_PROCESSES {
+            let idx = self.user_rr % MAX_PROCESSES;
+            self.user_rr = (self.user_rr + 1) % MAX_PROCESSES;
+            let p = &self.processes[idx];
+            if p.pid != 0
+                && p.state == ProcessState::Ready
+                && !p.resources_reaped
+                && p.context.rip != 0
+                && p.context.rsp != 0
+            {
+                return Some(p.pid);
+            }
+        }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// APIC timer preemption aday─▒: `after` d─▒┼ş─▒nda, daha ├Ânce kesme ile
+    /// kaydedilmi┼ş kullan─▒c─▒ ba─şlam─▒ (`kernel_rsp != 0`) olan `Ready` s├╝re├ğ.
+    /// Hen├╝z hi├ğ ├ğal─▒┼şmam─▒┼ş s├╝re├ğler burada d├Ând├╝r├╝lmez; onlar ├ğekirdek
+    /// taraf─▒ndaki `enter` yoluyla ba┼şlat─▒l─▒r.
+    pub fn next_user_process_after(&mut self, after: usize) -> Option<usize> {
+        for _ in 0..MAX_PROCESSES {
+            let idx = self.user_rr % MAX_PROCESSES;
+            self.user_rr = (self.user_rr + 1) % MAX_PROCESSES;
+            let p = &self.processes[idx];
+            if p.pid != 0
+                && p.pid != after
+                && p.state == ProcessState::Ready
+                && !p.resources_reaped
+                && p.context.kernel_rsp != 0
+            {
+                return Some(p.pid);
+            }
+        }
+        None
+    }
+
+    /// Bir sonraki calistirilabilir sureci doner (oncelikli Preemptive Round-Robin Zamanlayici).
+    /// Kullanici sureclerini oncelik sirasina (Realtime > High > Normal > Low) gore zamanlar,
+    /// calistirilabilir kullanici sureci yoksa PID 0 (kernel_core) fallback doner.
+    pub fn schedule_next(&mut self) -> Option<usize> {
+        // Sure dolmus engellenmis surecleri once uyandir (item 7).
+        let _ = self.wake_timeouts(crate::monotonic_ms());
+
+        if self.active_count == 0 {
+            return None;
+        }
+
+        // Mevcut calisan surecin slot indeksini bul
+        let curr_slot = self
+            .processes
+            .iter()
+            .position(|p| p.pid == self.current_pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused)
+            .unwrap_or(0);
+
+        // 1. once calistirilmaya hazir kullanici sureclerini oncelik sirasiyla ara (Realtime -> Low)
+        for target_prio in [
+            ProcessPriority::Realtime,
+            ProcessPriority::High,
+            ProcessPriority::Normal,
+            ProcessPriority::Low,
+        ] {
+            let total = MAX_PROCESSES;
+            for i in 1..=total {
+                let slot = (curr_slot + i) % total;
+                let p = &mut self.processes[slot];
+                if p.pid != 0
+                    && (p.state == ProcessState::Ready || p.state == ProcessState::Running)
+                    && p.priority == target_prio
+                {
+                    self.current_pid = p.pid;
+                    p.state = ProcessState::Running;
+                    p.cpu_time_ticks = p.cpu_time_ticks.saturating_add(1);
+                    return Some(p.pid);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // 2. Baska calistirilabilir kullanici sureci yoksa cekirdek ana surecine (PID 0) don
+        if self.processes[0].state == ProcessState::Running
+            || self.processes[0].state == ProcessState::Ready
+        {
+            self.current_pid = 0;
+            self.processes[0].state = ProcessState::Running;
+            self.processes[0].cpu_time_ticks = self.processes[0].cpu_time_ticks.saturating_add(1);
+            return Some(0);
+        }
+
+        Some(0) // kernel_core fallback
+    }
+
+    /// Get process reference by PID.
+    /// PID ile surec referansi alir.
+    pub fn get_process(&self, pid: usize) -> Option<&Process> {
+        self.processes
+            .iter()
+            .find(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused)
+    }
+
+    /// Activate the specified process's address space (switch CR3 with PCID).
+    /// This function should be called during context switch.
+    /// Belirtilen surecin adres uzay─▒n─▒ aktif eder (PCID ile CR3 de─şi┼ştirir).
+    /// Bu fonksiyon context switch s─▒ras─▒nda ├ğa─şr─▒lmal─▒d─▒r.
+    pub fn switch_address_space(&self, pid: usize) {
+        if let Some(p) = self.get_process(pid) {
+            if p.address_space.pml4_phys != 0 {
+                unsafe {
+                    p.address_space.load_cr3();
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Mevcut calisan surecin kayitli baglamini gunceller, yeni surecen CR3
+    /// degerini (`pml4 | pcid`) yukler ve kayitli yaymac durumunu geri yukler
+    /// (tam context switch). PCIDE a├ğ─▒kken `mov cr3` TLB flush ├╝retmedi─şinden
+    /// her ge├ği┼şte yazmak g├╝venli ve ucuzdur.
+    ///
+    /// # Safety
+    /// Donan─▒m kesmesi veya scheduler sinirlari cagrisinda kullanilir.
+    /// `next_pid` gecerli, calistirilir durumdaki bir surec olmalidir.
+    pub unsafe fn context_switch(&mut self, next_pid: usize) {
+        let current_pid = self.current_pid;
+
+        // Mevcut calisan surecin baglam─▒n─▒ kaydet (gerekirse)
+        if let Some(curr_p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == current_pid) {
+            if curr_p.state == ProcessState::Running {
+                curr_p.context = ProcessContext::save_current();
+                curr_p.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Engellenen veya sonlanmis bir sureci secmeyin; schedule_next zaten
+        // sadece Ready/Running secer, ancak guvenlik icin burada da kontrol edelim.
+        let next_ready = self
+            .get_process(next_pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Ready || p.state == ProcessState::Running);
+
+        if !next_ready {
+            // Calistirilebilir surec yok; kernel_core'a (PID 0) don.
+            return self.context_switch_locked(0);
+        }
+
+        // Adres uzayini ve baglami yeni surec icin hazirla.
+        // CR3 yaz─▒m─▒ `ctx.restore()` i├ğinde `cr3 | pcid` olarak yap─▒l─▒r;
+        // PCIDE a├ğ─▒kken `mov cr3` TLB flush ├╝retmedi─şinden s├╝re├ğ ge├ği┼şleri
+        // sayfa ├ğevirilerini PCID etiketleriyle korur. Burada ayr─▒ca
+        // `mov cr3` yapmak gereksiz ikinci bir yaz─▒m olurdu.
+        let next_context = self.get_process(next_pid).map(|p| p.context);
+
+        // Yeni s├╝rece ge├ğ
+        if let Some(next_p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == next_pid) {
+            self.current_pid = next_pid;
+            next_p.state = ProcessState::Running;
+            next_p.cpu_time_ticks = next_p.cpu_time_ticks.saturating_add(1);
+
+            // Kayitli baglami geri yukle ve sureci devam ettir (item 3).
+            // `restore` CR3'├╝ de do─şru PCID ile y├╝kler.
+            if let Some(ctx) = next_context {
+                ctx.restore();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `context_switch` icin kilitli (yeniden girilebilir olmayan) yardimci:
+    /// sadece CR3'yi ayarlar ve PID 0'a doner, restore cagirmayip geri doner.
+    unsafe fn context_switch_locked(&mut self, next_pid: usize) {
+        if let Some(next_p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == next_pid) {
+            self.current_pid = next_pid;
+            next_p.state = ProcessState::Running;
+            if let Some(pml4) = self
+                .get_process(next_pid)
+                .map(|p| p.address_space.pml4_phys)
+            {
+                if pml4 != 0 {
+                    AddressSpace::load_cr3_phys(pml4);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Sureci bir olay (veya mutlak sure dolmasi) uzerine engeller.
+    /// `pid` calistirilirken cagrilmalidir; surec Blocked durumuna alinir ve
+    /// `schedule_next` bunu atlar. `deadline_ms` verildiyse sure dolunca
+    /// `wake_timeouts` ile otomatik uyandirilir.
+    pub fn sleep(&mut self, pid: usize, event: usize, deadline_ms: Option<u64>) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if let Some(p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == pid) {
+            if p.state == ProcessState::Running || p.state == ProcessState::Ready {
+                p.state = ProcessState::Blocked;
+                p.wait_event = Some(event);
+                p.wait_deadline = deadline_ms;
+                true
+            } else {
+                false
+            }
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Belirli bir olaya bekleyen tum surecleri uyandirir ve calistirilir yapar.
+    /// Uyandirilan surec sayisini dondurur.
+    pub fn wake_event(&mut self, event: usize) -> usize {
+        let mut count = 0usize;
+        for p in self.processes.iter_mut() {
+            if p.state == ProcessState::Blocked && p.wait_event == Some(event) {
+                p.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+                p.wait_event = None;
+                p.wait_deadline = None;
+                count += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        count
+    }
+
+    /// Sure dolmus engellenmis surecleri uyandirir. `now_ms` guncel monotonik
+    /// zaman olmali; gecmis `wait_deadline` olan surecler Ready'ye doner.
+    /// Uyandirilan sayiyi dondurur.
+    pub fn wake_timeouts(&mut self, now_ms: u64) -> usize {
+        let mut count = 0usize;
+        for p in self.processes.iter_mut() {
+            if p.state == ProcessState::Blocked {
+                if let Some(deadline) = p.wait_deadline {
+                    if now_ms >= deadline {
+                        p.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+                        p.wait_event = None;
+                        p.wait_deadline = None;
+                        count += 1;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        count
+    }
+
+    /// Surec `Ready`/`Running` durumdaysa `Suspended`'a alir (SIGSTOP
+    /// semantigi). Zamanlayici `Suspended` surecleri secmez; kayitli kullanici
+    /// baglami `SIGCONT`'a kadar korunur. Calisan surecin kendisi de
+    /// duraklatilabilir ÔÇö bir sonraki zaman dilimi sinirinda baglami kaydedilir
+    /// ve surec bir daha secilmez. `Blocked`/`Terminated`/`Unused` icin
+    /// `false` doner (tek durum alani blocked+stopped birlesimini temsil
+    /// edemediginden bekleyen surecler simdilik duraklatilamaz).
+    pub fn suspend(&mut self, pid: usize) -> bool {
+        let Some(p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        match p.state {
+            ProcessState::Ready | ProcessState::Running => {
+                p.state = ProcessState::Suspended;
+                true
+            }
+            _ => false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `Suspended` sureci `Ready`'ye dondurur (SIGCONT semantigi). Durdurulmam─▒┼ş
+    /// s├╝re├ğlerde (Ready/Running/Blocked) zarars─▒z ba┼şar─▒ say─▒l─▒r; sonlanm─▒┼ş
+    /// veya var olmayan s├╝re├ğler `false` d├Âner.
+    pub fn resume(&mut self, pid: usize) -> bool {
