+        let Some(p) = self.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        match p.state {
+            ProcessState::Suspended => {
+                p.state = ProcessState::Ready;
+                true
+            }
+            // SIGCONT, durdurulmam─▒┼ş s├╝re├ğte zarars─▒zd─▒r (POSIX): Blocked
+            // s├╝re├ğler "stopped" say─▒lmad─▒─ş─▒ndan durumlar─▒ korunur.
+            ProcessState::Ready | ProcessState::Running | ProcessState::Blocked => true,
+            _ => false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `setpgid(2)`: s├╝reci bir s├╝re├ğ grubuna ta┼ş─▒r.
+    ///
+    /// `pgid == 0` hedefin kendi PID'si anlam─▒na gelir. Oturum lideri kendi
+    /// grubunu de─şi┼ştiremez ve ba┼şka oturumdaki bir gruba ge├ğilemez.
+    pub fn setpgid(&mut self, pid: usize, pgid: usize) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let target_pgid = if pgid == 0 { pid } else { pgid };
+        let Some(target) = self.get_process(pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let sid = target.sid;
+        if target.pgid == pid {
+            return false; // oturum/grup lideri
+        }
+        if target_pgid != pid {
+            let Some(leader) = self
+                .processes
+                .iter()
+                .find(|p| p.pid == target_pgid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused)
+            else {
+                return false;
+            };
+            if leader.sid != sid {
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+        if let Some(target) = self.get_process_mut(pid) {
+            target.pgid = target_pgid;
+            true
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `getpgid(2)`.
+    pub fn getpgid(&self, pid: usize) -> Option<usize> {
+        self.get_process(pid).map(|process| {
+            if process.pgid == 0 {
+                process.pid
+            } else {
+                process.pgid
+            }
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// `setsid(2)`: yeni bir oturum ve s├╝re├ğ grubu olu┼şturur.
+    pub fn setsid(&mut self, pid: usize) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let Some(target) = self.get_process(pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        // Grup lideri zaten oturum lideridir; POSIX buna izin vermez.
+        if target.pgid == pid {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if let Some(target) = self.get_process_mut(pid) {
+            target.sid = pid;
+            target.pgid = pid;
+            true
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `getsid(2)`.
+    pub fn getsid(&self, pid: usize) -> Option<usize> {
+        self.get_process(pid).map(|process| {
+            if process.sid == 0 {
+                process.pid
+            } else {
+                process.sid
+            }
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// `sched_setaffinity(2)`: s├╝recin ├ğal─▒┼şabilece─şi CPU k├╝mesini belirler.
+    ///
+    /// Maske `0` veya ├ğevrimi├ği olmayan bir CPU biti i├ğeriyorsa reddedilir.
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝re├ğleri ┼şu an BSP ├╝zerinde ├ğal─▒┼şt─▒─ş─▒ i├ğin API ileride SMP
+    /// kullan─▒c─▒ zamanlamas─▒ etkinle┼şti─şinde do─şrudan uygulanabilir.
+    pub fn set_affinity(&mut self, pid: usize, mask: u64) -> bool {
+        let cpus = crate::arch::x86_64::smp::cpu_count().clamp(1, 64) as u32;
+        let valid_mask = if cpus >= 64 {
+            u64::MAX
+        } else {
+            (1u64 << cpus) - 1
+        };
+        if pid == 0 || mask == 0 || (mask & !valid_mask) != 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if let Some(process) = self.get_process_mut(pid) {
+            process.affinity = mask;
+            true
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `sched_getaffinity(2)`: etkin CPU maskesi (`0` ÔåÆ t├╝m CPU'lar).
+    pub fn get_affinity(&self, pid: usize) -> Option<u64> {
+        let cpus = crate::arch::x86_64::smp::cpu_count().clamp(1, 64) as u32;
+        let all = if cpus >= 64 {
+            u64::MAX
+        } else {
+            (1u64 << cpus) - 1
+        };
+        self.get_process(pid).map(|process| {
+            if process.affinity == 0 {
+                all
+            } else {
+                process.affinity
+            }
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// S├╝re├ğ grubundaki t├╝m canl─▒ s├╝re├ğlere sinyal g├Ânderir.
+    /// D├Ân├╝┼ş: sinyalin ba┼şar─▒yla iletildi─şi s├╝re├ğ say─▒s─▒.
+    pub fn kill_group(&mut self, pgid: usize, sig: u64, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> usize {
+        if pgid == 0 || sig > signals::MAX_SIGNAL {
+            return 0;
+        }
+        let mut targets = [0usize; MAX_PROCESSES];
+        let mut count = 0;
+        for process in self.processes.iter() {
+            if process.pid != 0
+                && process.state != ProcessState::Unused
+                && process.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+                && process.pgid == pgid
+            {
+                targets[count] = process.pid;
+                count += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        let mut delivered = 0;
+        for &pid in targets.iter().take(count) {
+            if self.kill(pid, sig, pmm) {
+                delivered += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        delivered
+    }
+
+    /// Oturumdaki t├╝m canl─▒ s├╝re├ğlere sinyal g├Ânderir.
+    pub fn kill_session(&mut self, sid: usize, sig: u64, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> usize {
+        if sid == 0 || sig > signals::MAX_SIGNAL {
+            return 0;
+        }
+        let mut targets = [0usize; MAX_PROCESSES];
+        let mut count = 0;
+        for process in self.processes.iter() {
+            if process.pid != 0
+                && process.state != ProcessState::Unused
+                && process.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+                && process.sid == sid
+            {
+                targets[count] = process.pid;
+                count += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        let mut delivered = 0;
+        for &pid in targets.iter().take(count) {
+            if self.kill(pid, sig, pmm) {
+                delivered += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        delivered
+    }
+
+    /// Bekleyen sinyaller i├ğinde varsay─▒lan eylemi olanlar─▒ uygular.
+    ///
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicisi kaydedilmi┼ş sinyaller beklemede b─▒rak─▒l─▒r
+    /// (`SYS_SIGACTION` ile kay─▒t yap─▒labilir). ├ûl├╝mc├╝l sinyaller s├╝reci
+    /// `-(sig)` ├ğ─▒k─▒┼ş koduyla sonland─▒r─▒r; durdurma sinyalleri `Suspended`
+    /// yapar. En az bir eylem uyguland─▒ysa `true` d├Âner.
+    pub fn deliver_pending_defaults(
+        &mut self,
+        pid: usize,
+        pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+    ) -> bool {
+        let Some(process) = self.get_process(pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let pending = process.pending_signals & !process.signal_mask;
+        if pending == 0 {
+            return false;
+        }
+        // ─░lk uygulanabilir sinyali se├ğ: kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicisi kay─▒tl─▒
+        // sinyaller beklemede kal─▒r. Se├ğim d├Âng├╝s├╝ `self` ├╝zerinde salt-okunur
+        // kal─▒r; eylem uygulamas─▒ ayr─▒ ad─▒mda yap─▒l─▒r (bor├ğ ├ğak─▒┼şmas─▒ yok).
+        let mut chosen = None;
+        for sig in 1..=signals::MAX_SIGNAL {
+            let bit = 1u64 << (sig - 1);
+            if pending & bit == 0 {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let handler = process
+                .signal_handlers
+                .get((sig - 1) as usize)
+                .copied()
+                .unwrap_or(0);
+            let action = match handler {
+                0 => default_signal_action(sig),
+                // `SIG_IGN`: bekleyen sinyal sessizce d├╝┼ş├╝r├╝l├╝r.
+                1 => SignalAction::Ignore,
+                // Kay─▒tl─▒ kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicisi: sinyal beklemede kal─▒r.
+                _ => continue,
+            };
+            chosen = Some((sig, action));
+            break;
+        }
+        let Some((sig, action)) = chosen else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        let bit = 1u64 << (sig - 1);
+        if let Some(process) = self.get_process_mut(pid) {
+            process.pending_signals &= !bit;
+        }
+        match action {
+            SignalAction::Terminate => {
+                self.terminate(pid, -(sig as i32), pmm);
+            }
+            SignalAction::Stop => {
+                self.suspend(pid);
+            }
+            SignalAction::Continue => {
+                self.resume(pid);
+            }
+            SignalAction::Ignore => {}
+        }
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// `SYS_SIGACTION`: kullan─▒c─▒ sinyal i┼şleyicisi kaydeder.
+    ///
+    /// `SIGKILL` ve `SIGSTOP` i├ğin i┼şleyici atanamaz. `handler == 0` varsay─▒lan
+    /// eyleme d├Ân├╝┼ş anlam─▒na gelir; `handler == 1` sinyali yok sayar
+    /// (`SIG_IGN`).
+    pub fn set_signal_handler(&mut self, pid: usize, sig: u64, handler: u64) -> bool {
+        if sig == 0
+            || sig > signals::MAX_SIGNAL
+            || matches!(sig, signals::SIGKILL | signals::SIGSTOP)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+        // `handler` ileride kullan─▒c─▒ kipinde ├ğa─şr─▒lacak bir adrestir;
+        // ├ğekirdek adresi veya non-canonical de─şer kabul edilirse teslimat
+        // an─▒nda ring0'da #GP/#PF ├╝retir. 0 (varsay─▒lan) ve 1 (SIG_IGN)
+        // ayr─▒lm─▒┼ş de─şerlerdir; di─şerleri kanonik kullan─▒c─▒ yar─▒s─▒nda olmal─▒.
+        if handler >= crate::mm::uaccess::USER_ADDRESS_END {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let Some(process) = self.get_process_mut(pid) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        process.signal_handlers[(sig - 1) as usize] = handler;
+        // SIG_IGN: bekleyen ayn─▒ sinyali d├╝┼ş├╝r.
+        if handler == 1 {
+            process.pending_signals &= !(1u64 << (sig - 1));
+        }
+        true
+    }
+
+    /// `SYS_SIGPROCMASK`: sinyal maskesini ayarlar. `how`: 0 = kapat, 1 = a├ğ,
+    /// 2 = de─şi┼ştir. D├Ân├╝┼ş: ├Ânceki maske.
+    pub fn set_signal_mask(&mut self, pid: usize, how: u64, mask: u64) -> Option<u64> {
+        let process = self.get_process_mut(pid)?;
+        let old = process.signal_mask;
+        let mask = mask & !(1u64 << (signals::SIGKILL - 1)) & !(1u64 << (signals::SIGSTOP - 1));
+        process.signal_mask = match how {
+            0 => old & !mask,
+            1 => old | mask,
+            2 => mask,
+            _ => return None,
+        };
+        Some(old)
+    }
+
+    /// `kill(2)` semantigi: `sig` hedef surece sinyal gonderir.
+    ///
+    /// * `pid == 0` (cekirdek) veya gecersiz `sig` (> `signals::MAX_SIGNAL`):
+    ///   `false`.
+    /// * `sig == 0`: varlik denetimi ÔÇö surec varsa `true`.
+    /// * `SIGKILL` / `SIGTERM`: surec `-(sig)` cikis koduyla sonlandirilir.
+    /// * `SIGSTOP`: `Ready`/`Running` surec `Suspended`'a alinir.
+    /// * `SIGCONT`: `Suspended` surec `Ready`'ye doner.
+    /// * Diger sinyaller `pending_signals` bit maskesine islenir; kullanici
+    ///   isleyicisine teslimat sonraki sinyal altyapisi maddesinde yapilacak.
+    pub fn kill(&mut self, pid: usize, sig: u64, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+        if pid == 0 || sig > signals::MAX_SIGNAL {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let exists = self.processes.iter().any(|p| {
+            p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Unused && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+        });
+        if !exists {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if sig == 0 {
+            return true;
+        }
+        match sig {
+            signals::SIGKILL | signals::SIGTERM => self.terminate(pid, -(sig as i32), pmm),
+            signals::SIGSTOP => self.suspend(pid),
+            signals::SIGCONT => self.resume(pid),
+            _ => {
+                if let Some(p) = self
+                    .processes
+                    .iter_mut()
+                    .find(|p| p.pid == pid && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated)
+                {
+                    p.pending_signals |= 1u64 << (sig - 1);
+                    true
+                } else {
+                    false
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Default for ProcessManager {
+    fn default() -> Self {
+        Self::new()
+    }
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// System Call Interface / Sistem cagrisi (Syscall) Arabirimi
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+/// Standard System Call Numbers
+/// Standart Sistem cagrisi Numaralari
+pub mod sys_nos {
+    pub const SYS_YIELD: u64 = 0;
+    pub const SYS_EXIT: u64 = 1;
+    pub const SYS_GETPID: u64 = 2;
+    pub const SYS_ALLOC_FRAME: u64 = 3;
+    pub const SYS_FREE_FRAME: u64 = 4;
+    pub const SYS_WRITE: u64 = 5;
+    pub const SYS_READ: u64 = 6;
+    pub const SYS_SPAWN: u64 = 7;
+    pub const SYS_EXEC: u64 = 8;
+    pub const SYS_FORK: u64 = 9;
+    pub const SYS_WAIT: u64 = 10;
+    /// Heap ucunu ayarlar: RDI = HEAP_BASE'e g├Âre yeni u├ğ (bayt). D├Ân├╝┼ş: yeni u├ğ.
+    pub const SYS_BRK: u64 = 11;
+    /// Anonim mmap: RDI = uzunluk, RSI = bayraklar. D├Ân├╝┼ş: adres (0 = hata).
+    pub const SYS_MMAP: u64 = 12;
+    /// mmap b├Âlgesini b─▒rak─▒r: RDI = adres, RSI = uzunluk. D├Ân├╝┼ş: 0 / u64::MAX.
+    pub const SYS_MUNMAP: u64 = 13;
+    /// Kullan─▒c─▒ bellek ├Âzeti: [0] heap_brk, [1] mmap b├Âlge say─▒s─▒,
+    /// [2] mmap bayt, [3] e┼şlenmi┼ş heap sayfas─▒ (ABI uyumlu ilk 4 alan).
+    /// Geni┼şletilmi┼ş alanlar: [4] kullan─▒c─▒ ├ğer├ğevesi, [5] payla┼ş─▒lan (COW)
+    /// page, index 6 = shared bytes gained, index 7 = system total bytes,
+    /// [8] sistem bo┼ş bayt, [9] sistem kullan─▒c─▒ ├ğer├ğevesi,
+    /// [10] sistem kernel ├ğer├ğevesi, [11] kernel heap haritalanan bayt.
+    /// RDI = ├ğ─▒kt─▒ tamponu (u64 x 12 = 96 bayt).
+    pub const SYS_MEMINFO: u64 = 14;
+    /// Dosya a├ğar: RDI = yol (`ptr`, NUL sonlu), RSI = bayraklar. D├Ân├╝┼ş: fd / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_OPEN: u64 = 15;
+    /// fd kapat─▒r: RDI = fd. D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_CLOSE: u64 = 16;
+    /// Kaynak kullan─▒m─▒/limit raporu: RDI = ├ğ─▒kt─▒ tamponu (u64 x 8).
+    pub const SYS_RESINFO: u64 = 17;
+    /// fd konumland─▒rma: RDI = fd, RSI = offset (i64), RDX = whence (0/1/2).
+    pub const SYS_LSEEK: u64 = 18;
+    /// S├╝rece sinyal g├Ânderir / s├╝reci kontrol eder: RDI = pid, RSI = sinyal
+    /// numaras─▒. SIGKILL/SIGTERM sonland─▒r─▒r, SIGSTOP/SIGCONT duraklat─▒r ve
+    /// s├╝rd├╝r├╝r, `sig == 0` varl─▒k denetimidir. Di─şer sinyaller
+    /// `pending_signals`'e i┼şlenir. Negatif `pid` s├╝re├ğ grubunu hedefler.
+    /// D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_KILL: u64 = 19;
+    /// S├╝re├ğ grubunu ayarlar: RDI = pid (`0` = ├ğa─ş─▒ran), RSI = pgid
+    /// (`0` = kendi PID'si). D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SETPGID: u64 = 20;
+    /// S├╝re├ğ grubunu d├Ând├╝r├╝r: RDI = pid (`0` = ├ğa─ş─▒ran). D├Ân├╝┼ş: pgid / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_GETPGID: u64 = 21;
+    /// Yeni oturum a├ğar: RDI yok say─▒l─▒r, ├ğa─ş─▒ran oturum lideri olur.
+    /// D├Ân├╝┼ş: yeni sid / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SETSID: u64 = 22;
+    /// Oturum kimli─şini d├Ând├╝r├╝r: RDI = pid (`0` = ├ğa─ş─▒ran). D├Ân├╝┼ş: sid / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_GETSID: u64 = 23;
+    /// CPU yak─▒nl─▒k maskesini ayarlar: RDI = pid (`0` = ├ğa─ş─▒ran),
+    /// RSI = maske (bit `i` = CPU `i`). D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY: u64 = 24;
+    /// CPU yak─▒nl─▒k maskesini d├Ând├╝r├╝r: RDI = pid (`0` = ├ğa─ş─▒ran).
+    /// D├Ân├╝┼ş: maske / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SCHED_GETAFFINITY: u64 = 25;
+    /// Sinyal i┼şleyicisi kaydeder: RDI = sinyal, RSI = i┼şleyici adresi
+    /// (`0` = varsay─▒lan, `1` = yok say). D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SIGACTION: u64 = 26;
+    /// Sinyal maskesini de─şi┼ştirir: RDI = nas─▒l (`0` kapat, `1` a├ğ,
+    /// `2` de─şi┼ştir), RSI = maske bitleri. D├Ân├╝┼ş: ├Ânceki maske / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SIGPROCMASK: u64 = 27;
+    /// Seccomp-tarz─▒ syscall filtresi: RDI = yeni izin maskesi (bit `n` =
+    /// syscall `n` serbest). Yaln─▒zca daraltma kabul edilir
+    /// (`yeni = eski & arg1`); geni┼şletme imk├óns─▒zd─▒r. `SYS_EXIT` her zaman
+    /// serbesttir. D├Ân├╝┼ş: `0` / `MAX`.
+    pub const SYS_SECCOMP: u64 = 28;
+}
+
+/// Standart sinyal numaralar─▒ (Linux numaraland─▒rmas─▒yla uyumlu alt k├╝me).
+/// `kill` syscall'─▒ ve `Process::pending_signals` bu de─şerleri kullan─▒r.
+pub mod signals {
+    pub const SIGHUP: u64 = 1;
+    pub const SIGINT: u64 = 2;
+    pub const SIGQUIT: u64 = 3;
+    pub const SIGILL: u64 = 4;
+    pub const SIGTRAP: u64 = 5;
+    pub const SIGABRT: u64 = 6;
+    pub const SIGBUS: u64 = 7;
+    pub const SIGFPE: u64 = 8;
+    pub const SIGKILL: u64 = 9;
+    pub const SIGUSR1: u64 = 10;
+    pub const SIGSEGV: u64 = 11;
+    pub const SIGUSR2: u64 = 12;
+    pub const SIGPIPE: u64 = 13;
+    pub const SIGALRM: u64 = 14;
+    pub const SIGTERM: u64 = 15;
+    pub const SIGSTKFLT: u64 = 16;
+    pub const SIGCHLD: u64 = 17;
+    pub const SIGCONT: u64 = 18;
+    pub const SIGSTOP: u64 = 19;
+    pub const SIGTSTP: u64 = 20;
+    pub const SIGTTIN: u64 = 21;
+    pub const SIGTTOU: u64 = 22;
+    /// ─░┼şlenebilir sinyal bit maskesinin ├╝st s─▒n─▒r─▒ (64 bit).
+    pub const MAX_SIGNAL: u64 = 64;
+}
+
+/// Kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicisi kay─▒tl─▒ de─şilken uygulanan varsay─▒lan sinyal eylemi.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum SignalAction {
+    /// S├╝reci `-(sig)` ├ğ─▒k─▒┼ş koduyla sonland─▒r─▒r.
+    Terminate,
+    /// S├╝reci `Suspended` durumuna al─▒r.
+    Stop,
+    /// Duraklat─▒lm─▒┼ş s├╝reci s├╝rd├╝r├╝r.
+    Continue,
+    /// Sinyali sessizce d├╝┼ş├╝r├╝r.
+    Ignore,
+}
+
+/// Linux numaraland─▒rmas─▒yla uyumlu varsay─▒lan sinyal eylemi tablosu.
+pub const fn default_signal_action(sig: u64) -> SignalAction {
+    match sig {
+        signals::SIGHUP
+        | signals::SIGINT
+        | signals::SIGQUIT
+        | signals::SIGILL
+        | signals::SIGTRAP
+        | signals::SIGABRT
+        | signals::SIGBUS
+        | signals::SIGFPE
+        | signals::SIGKILL
+        | signals::SIGUSR1
+        | signals::SIGSEGV
+        | signals::SIGUSR2
+        | signals::SIGPIPE
+        | signals::SIGALRM
+        | signals::SIGTERM
+        | signals::SIGSTKFLT => SignalAction::Terminate,
+        signals::SIGSTOP | signals::SIGTSTP | signals::SIGTTIN | signals::SIGTTOU => {
+            SignalAction::Stop
+        }
+        signals::SIGCONT => SignalAction::Continue,
+        _ => SignalAction::Ignore,
+    }
+}
+
+pub const MAX_EXEC_IMAGE_SIZE: usize = 112 * 1024;
+
+fn copy_user_bytes(process: &Process, address: u64, len: usize) -> Option<alloc::vec::Vec<u8>> {
+    uaccess::copy_from_user(&process.address_space, address, len).ok()
+}
+
+/// System call dispatcher (test/├ğekirdek i├ği kolayl─▒k sarmalay─▒c─▒s─▒).
+/// `arg3` = RDX (3. arg├╝man); 4. arg├╝man (`R10`) yok say─▒l─▒r.
+/// Ayr─▒nt─▒l─▒ ABI i├ğin `syscall_dispatch_abi` kullan─▒n.
+/// Sistem cagrisi sevkedicisi (Syscall Dispatcher).
+/// x86_64 ABI:
+/// RAX = Syscall No
+/// RDI = Arg 1
+/// RSI = Arg 2
+/// RDX = Arg 3
+/// R10 = Arg 4
+/// Donen deger: RAX (Sonuc kodu / adres)
+pub fn syscall_dispatch(
+    sys_no: u64,
+    arg1: u64,
+    arg2: u64,
+    arg3: u64,
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> u64 {
+    syscall_dispatch_abi(sys_no, arg1, arg2, arg3, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+}
+
+/// Tam ABI sevkedicisi: RDX (`arg3`) ve R10 (`arg4`) ayr─▒ ta┼ş─▒n─▒r.
+pub fn syscall_dispatch_abi(
+    sys_no: u64,
+    arg1: u64,
+    arg2: u64,
+    arg3: u64,
+    arg4: u64,
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> u64 {
+    let _ = arg4;
+
+    // Seccomp-tarz─▒ izin maskesi: s├╝recin `syscall_mask`'i `u64::MAX`'ten
+    // farkl─▒ysa yaln─▒zca maskede biti set olan syscall'ler ├ğal─▒┼ş─▒r.
+    // `SYS_EXIT` her zaman serbesttir; aksi halde filtrelenmi┼ş bir s├╝re├ğ
+    // sonland─▒r─▒lamaz hale gelebilir. 64 ve ├╝zeri numaralar maskeye s─▒─şmaz,
+    // filtreli s├╝re├ğte do─şrudan reddedilir.
+    if let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(proc_mgr.current_pid) {
+        let mask = process.syscall_mask;
+        if mask != u64::MAX
+            && sys_no != sys_nos::SYS_EXIT
+            && (sys_no >= 64 || (mask >> sys_no) & 1 == 0)
+        {
+            return u64::MAX;
+        }
+    }
+
+    match sys_no {
+        sys_nos::SYS_YIELD => {
+            proc_mgr.schedule_next();
+            0
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_EXIT => {
+            let exit_code = arg1 as i32;
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            proc_mgr.mark_exited(current, exit_code);
+            0
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_GETPID => proc_mgr.current_pid as u64,
+        sys_nos::SYS_ALLOC_FRAME => {
+            if let Some(frame) = pmm.allocate_frame() {
+                let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+                if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == current) {
+                    if p.resources.record_user_frame(frame) {
+                        return frame;
+                    }
+                }
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+                0
+            } else {
+                0
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_FREE_FRAME => {
+            let frame = arg1;
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let owned = proc_mgr
+                .processes
+                .iter_mut()
+                .find(|p| p.pid == current)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.resources.take_user_frame(frame));
+            if owned {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE => {
+            let len = arg2 as usize;
+            if len == 0 || len > 1024 {
+                return 0;
+            }
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(proc_mgr.current_pid) else {
+                return 0;
+            };
+            let Some(buffer) = copy_user_bytes(process, arg1, len) else {
+                return 0;
+            };
+            if let Ok(text) = core::str::from_utf8(&buffer) {
+                crate::serial::print(text);
+            }
+            len as u64
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_FORK => proc_mgr
+            .fork_process(proc_mgr.current_pid, pmm)
+            .map(|pid| pid as u64)
+            .unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
+        sys_nos::SYS_WAIT => {
+            let parent_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let child_pid = (arg1 != 0).then_some(arg1 as usize);
+            if arg2 != 0 {
+                let Some(parent) = proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid) else {
+                    return u64::MAX;
+                };
+                if uaccess::validate_user_range(
+                    &parent.address_space,
+                    arg2,
+                    core::mem::size_of::<i32>(),
+                    true,
+                )
+                .is_err()
+                {
+                    return u64::MAX;
+                }
+            }
+            match proc_mgr.wait_child(parent_pid, child_pid, pmm) {
+                Ok(Some((pid, exit_code))) => {
+                    if arg2 != 0 {
+                        let Some(parent) = proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid) else {
+                            return u64::MAX;
+                        };
+                        if !ProcessManager::write_wait_status(parent, arg2, exit_code) {
+                            return u64::MAX;
+                        }
+                    }
+                    pid as u64
+                }
+                Ok(None) => {
+                    let Some(parent) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(parent_pid) else {
+                        return u64::MAX;
+                    };
+                    parent.state = ProcessState::Blocked;
+                    parent.waiting_child = Some(child_pid.unwrap_or(0));
+                    parent.wait_status_addr = arg2;
+                    parent.context.rax = 0;
+                    parent.has_saved_user_context = true;
+                    0
+                }
+                Err(_) => u64::MAX,
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_EXEC => {
+            let len = arg2 as usize;
+            if len == 0 || len > MAX_EXEC_IMAGE_SIZE {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let Some(image) = copy_user_bytes(process, arg1, len) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            if proc_mgr.exec_process(current, &image, pmm).is_ok() {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_BRK => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            crate::mm::user_mem::brk(process, arg1, pmm)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_MMAP => {
+            if arg1 == 0 || arg1 > 64 * 1024 * 1024 {
+                return 0;
+            }
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return 0;
+            };
+            crate::mm::user_mem::mmap(process, arg1, arg2, pmm).unwrap_or(0)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP => {
+            if arg1 == 0 || arg2 == 0 {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            if crate::mm::user_mem::munmap(process, arg1, arg2, pmm) {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            // Sistem geneli profil (salt okuma; s├╝re├ğ tablosu zaten kilitli).
+            let system = crate::mm::memory::profile::snapshot();
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let stats = crate::mm::user_mem::stats(process);
+            let extended = stats.extended(process);
+            let address_space = process.address_space;
+            // ─░lk 4 alan ABI uyumlulu─şu i├ğin korunur; 5..12 geni┼şletilmi┼ş
+            // kullan─▒c─▒/kernel istatistikleridir (bkz. docs/SYSCALL_ABI.md).
+            let report = [
+                stats.heap_bytes,
+                stats.mmap_regions as u64,
+                stats.mmap_bytes,
+                stats.heap_mapped_pages as u64,
+                extended.user_frame_count as u64,
+                extended.shared_pages as u64,
+                extended.shared_saved_bytes,
+                system.total_bytes,
+                system.free_bytes,
+                system.user_frames as u64,
+                system.kernel_frames as u64,
+                system.heap_mapped_bytes,
+            ];
+            if arg1 == 0 {
+                return report
+                    .iter()
+                    .copied()
+                    .fold(0u64, |acc, v| acc ^ v.rotate_left(7));
+            }
+            let mut bytes = [0u8; 96];
+            for (index, value) in report.iter().enumerate() {
+                bytes[index * 8..index * 8 + 8].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
+            }
+            // ├ç─▒kt─▒ tamponu tembel sayfaya d├╝┼şebilir; ├Ânce ger├ğekleyin.
+            proc_mgr.realize_lazy_range(current, arg1, 96, pmm);
+            if uaccess::copy_to_user(&address_space, arg1, &bytes).is_err() {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            0
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_OPEN => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let Ok(path_bytes) =
+                uaccess::copy_cstring_from_user(&process.address_space, arg1, fd::MAX_FD_PATH)
+            else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            if path_bytes.is_empty() {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let Ok(path) = core::str::from_utf8(&path_bytes) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let flags = arg2 as u32;
+            let Ok(object) = proc_mgr.files.open(path, flags) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let allocation = {
+                let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                    return u64::MAX;
+                };
+                let fd_limit = process.resources.limits.max_fds.min(fd::MAX_FDS);
+                if fd::used_fd_count(&process.fds) >= fd_limit {
+                    Err(())
+                } else {
+                    fd::alloc_fd(&mut process.fds, object, flags).map_err(|_| ())
+                }
+            };
+            match allocation {
+                Ok(fd_index) => fd_index as u64,
+                Err(()) => {
+                    proc_mgr.files.release(object);
+                    u64::MAX
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE => {
+            let fd_index = arg1 as usize;
+            if !(fd::FIRST_USER_FD..fd::MAX_FDS).contains(&fd_index) {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            match fd::close_fd(&mut process.fds, fd_index) {
+                Some(object) => {
+                    proc_mgr.files.release(object);
+                    0
+                }
+                None => u64::MAX,
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_READ => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let fd_index = arg1 as usize;
+            let buffer_address = arg2;
+            let len = arg3 as usize;
+            if len == 0 {
+                return 0;
+            }
+            if len > uaccess::MAX_USER_COPY {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let descriptor = {
+                let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                    return u64::MAX;
+                };
+                match process.fds.get(fd_index) {
+                    Some(entry) if entry.used => *entry,
+                    _ => return u64::MAX,
+                }
+            };
+            if descriptor.kind == FileKind::Stdin {
+                return 0;
+            }
+            if descriptor.kind != FileKind::Regular {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let mut buffer = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+            if buffer.try_reserve_exact(len).is_err() {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            buffer.resize(len, 0);
+            let read =
+                proc_mgr
+                    .files
+                    .read_at(descriptor.object as usize, descriptor.offset, &mut buffer);
+            // Hedef tampon tembel sayfalara d├╝┼şebilir; ├Ânce ger├ğekleyin.
+            proc_mgr.realize_lazy_range(current, buffer_address, read as u64, pmm);
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let address_space = process.address_space;
+            if uaccess::copy_to_user(&address_space, buffer_address, &buffer[..read]).is_err() {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            if let Some(entry) = process.fds.get_mut(fd_index) {
+                entry.offset = entry.offset.saturating_add(read as u64);
+            }
+            read as u64
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_LSEEK => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let fd_index = arg1 as usize;
+            let delta = arg2 as i64;
+            let whence = arg3;
+            let descriptor = {
+                let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                    return u64::MAX;
+                };
+                match process.fds.get(fd_index) {
+                    Some(entry) if entry.used => *entry,
+                    _ => return u64::MAX,
+                }
+            };
+            if descriptor.kind != FileKind::Regular {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let Some(size) = proc_mgr.files.object_size(descriptor.object as usize) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let base = match whence {
+                0 => 0i128,
+                1 => descriptor.offset as i128,
+                2 => size as i128,
+                _ => return u64::MAX,
+            };
+            let target = base + delta as i128;
+            if target < 0 || target > size as i128 {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let Some(entry) = process.fds.get_mut(fd_index) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            entry.offset = target as u64;
+            target as u64
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_KILL => {
+            // Negatif pid s├╝re├ğ grubunu hedefler (POSIX `kill(-pgid, sig)`).
+            let target = arg1 as i64;
+            if target < 0 {
+                let pgid = target.unsigned_abs() as usize;
+                if proc_mgr.kill_group(pgid, arg2, pmm) > 0 {
+                    0
+                } else {
+                    u64::MAX
+                }
+            } else if proc_mgr.kill(arg1 as usize, arg2, pmm) {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SETPGID => {
+            let target = if arg1 == 0 {
+                proc_mgr.current_pid
+            } else {
+                arg1 as usize
+            };
+            if proc_mgr.setpgid(target, arg2 as usize) {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_GETPGID => {
+            let target = if arg1 == 0 {
+                proc_mgr.current_pid
+            } else {
+                arg1 as usize
+            };
+            proc_mgr
+                .getpgid(target)
+                .map(|pgid| pgid as u64)
+                .unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SETSID => {
+            let pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            if proc_mgr.setsid(pid) {
+                pid as u64
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_GETSID => {
+            let target = if arg1 == 0 {
+                proc_mgr.current_pid
+            } else {
+                arg1 as usize
+            };
+            proc_mgr
+                .getsid(target)
+                .map(|sid| sid as u64)
+                .unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY => {
+            let target = if arg1 == 0 {
+                proc_mgr.current_pid
+            } else {
+                arg1 as usize
+            };
+            if proc_mgr.set_affinity(target, arg2) {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_GETAFFINITY => {
+            let target = if arg1 == 0 {
+                proc_mgr.current_pid
+            } else {
+                arg1 as usize
+            };
+            proc_mgr.get_affinity(target).unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SIGACTION => {
+            let pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            if proc_mgr.set_signal_handler(pid, arg1, arg2) {
+                0
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            }
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SIGPROCMASK => {
+            let pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            proc_mgr
+                .set_signal_mask(pid, arg1, arg2)
+                .unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_SECCOMP => {
+            // Yaln─▒zca daraltma: mevcut maskeyle AND'lenir, b├Âylece bir
+            // s├╝re├ğ kendini bir kez k─▒s─▒tlad─▒ktan sonra filtreyi geri alamaz.
+            // `SYS_EXIT` biti maskede olmasa bile her zaman ├ğa─şr─▒labilir.
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            process.syscall_mask &= arg1;
+            0
+        }
+        sys_nos::SYS_RESINFO => {
+            let current = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+            let Some(process) = proc_mgr.get_process(current) else {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            };
+            let stats = crate::mm::user_mem::stats(process);
+            let address_space = process.address_space;
+            let report = [
+                process.resources.limits.max_frames as u64,
+                process.resources.frame_count as u64,
+                process.resources.limits.max_user_frames as u64,
+                process.resources.user_frame_count as u64,
+                process.resources.limits.max_fds as u64,
+                fd::used_fd_count(&process.fds) as u64,
+                process.resources.limits.max_mmap_bytes,
+                stats.mmap_bytes,
+            ];
+            let mut bytes = [0u8; 64];
+            for (index, value) in report.iter().enumerate() {
+                bytes[index * 8..index * 8 + 8].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
+            }
+            // ├ç─▒kt─▒ tamponu tembel sayfaya d├╝┼şebilir; ├Ânce ger├ğekleyin.
+            proc_mgr.realize_lazy_range(current, arg1, 64, pmm);
+            if uaccess::copy_to_user(&address_space, arg1, &bytes).is_err() {
+                return u64::MAX;
+            }
+            0
+        }
+        _ => u64::MAX, // Desteklenmeyen cagri
+    }
+}
+
+/// Kullan─▒c─▒ adresine 8 bayt yazar; hedef sayfa yaz─▒labilir (`WRITABLE`) ve
+/// kullan─▒c─▒ eri┼şimli de─şilse reddeder. Salt-okunur kod sayfas─▒na yazma
+/// giri┼şimi `false` d├Âner (├ğekirdek #PF ├╝retmez).
+pub fn write_user_u64(process: &Process, address: u64, value: u64) -> bool {
+    uaccess::write_user_u64(&process.address_space, address, value).is_ok()
+}
+
+/// Test process lifecycle, crash/termination, invalid syscalls, and resource cleanup.
+/// Surec yasam dongusu, kaza (crash/termination), gecersiz sistem cagrisi ve kaynak temizleme testi.
+pub fn test_process_lifecycle_and_fault_recovery(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print(
+        "[PROC-TEST] --- S├╝re├ğ ├ğ├Âkmesi, syscall hatalar─▒ ve kaynak temizleme testi ---\n",
+    );
+
+    let initial_free = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let initial_active = proc_mgr.active_count;
+
+    // 1. Yeni bir test sureci baslat (spawn)
+    let pid = match proc_mgr.spawn("fault_test_proc", 0x1000, 0x7000_2000, 99, Some(99), pmm) {
+        Ok(id) => id,
+        Err(_) => {
+            crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] S├╝re├ğ ba┼şlatma: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+            return false;
+        }
+    };
+    crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] 1. Test s├╝reci ba┼şlat─▒ld─▒ (PID ");
+    crate::serial::print_dec(pid as u64);
+    crate::serial_print("): BA┼ŞARILI\n");
+
+    // 2. Surece kaynak tahsis et (PMM cerceveleri)
+    let frame1 = match pmm.allocate_frame() {
+        Some(f) => f,
+        None => return false,
+    };
+    let frame2 = match pmm.allocate_frame() {
+        Some(f) => f,
+        None => {
+            pmm.free_frame(frame1);
+            return false;
+        }
+    };
+    if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == pid) {
+        p.resources.record_frame(frame1);
+        p.resources.record_frame(frame2);
+    }
+    crate::serial_print(
+        "[PROC-TEST] 2. S├╝rece 2 sayfa ├ğer├ğevesi tahsis edildi (Kaynak takibi aktif)\n",
+    );
+
+    // 3. Hatali / Gecersiz Sistem cagrisi Testi (Gecersiz Syscall No)
+    let invalid_res = syscall_dispatch(9999, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let null_ptr_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, 0, 100, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+    let syscall_frame = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_ALLOC_FRAME, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let owned_free = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_FREE_FRAME, syscall_frame, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let owned_free_succeeded = syscall_frame != 0 && owned_free == 0;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    let free_before_unowned = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let unowned_free = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_FREE_FRAME, frame2, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let unowned_free_rejected =
+        unowned_free == u64::MAX && pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before_unowned;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    if invalid_res == u64::MAX
+        && null_ptr_write == 0
+        && owned_free_succeeded
+        && unowned_free_rejected
+    {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PROC-TEST] 3. Ge├ğersiz syscall, kullan─▒c─▒ tamponu ve sahiplik s─▒n─▒rlar─▒: BA┼ŞARILI\n",
+        );
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] Hatal─▒ sistem ├ğa─şr─▒s─▒ s─▒n─▒r korumas─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // 4. Surec cokmesi / Hata Simulasyonu (SIGSEGV / Fault Exit Code -11)
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, -11, pmm);
+    if terminated {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PROC-TEST] 4. S├╝re├ğ ├ğ├Âkmesi sim├╝le edildi (Exit Code: -11 / SIGSEGV)\n",
+        );
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] S├╝re├ğ sonland─▒rma: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // 5. Kaynak Temizligi ve Bellek Sizintisi Kontrolu
+    let free_after = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let active_after = proc_mgr.active_count;
+
+    if free_after != initial_free || active_after != initial_active {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] Kaynak temizleme s─▒z─▒nt─▒s─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+    crate::serial_print(
+        "[PROC-TEST] 5. S├╝re├ğ kaynaklar─▒ PMM'e eksiksiz iade edildi (s─▒z─▒nt─▒: 0 bayt): BA┼ŞARILI\n",
+    );
+
+    // 6. oncelikli Zamanlayici (Priority Preemptive Scheduler) Testi
+    let p_low = proc_mgr
+        .spawn("low_proc", 0x1000, 0x7000_1000, 1, None, pmm)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let p_high = proc_mgr
+        .spawn("high_proc", 0x2000, 0x7000_2000, 1, None, pmm)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == p_high) {
+        p.priority = ProcessPriority::High;
+    }
+    if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.processes.iter_mut().find(|p| p.pid == p_low) {
+        p.priority = ProcessPriority::Low;
+    }
+
+    let next_pid = proc_mgr.schedule_next();
+    let sched_ok = next_pid == Some(p_high);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(p_low, 0, pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(p_high, 0, pmm);
+
+    // 7. Esit onemli surecler icin round-robin dogrulamasi.
+    let rr_a = proc_mgr
+        .spawn("rr_a", 0x3000, 0x7000_3000, 1, None, pmm)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let rr_b = proc_mgr
+        .spawn("rr_b", 0x4000, 0x7000_4000, 1, None, pmm)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let first = proc_mgr.schedule_next();
+    let second = proc_mgr.schedule_next();
+    let rr_ok = matches!(first, Some(x) if x == rr_a || x == rr_b)
+        && matches!(second, Some(y) if y == rr_a || y == rr_b)
+        && first != second;
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(rr_a, 0, pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(rr_b, 0, pmm);
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    let lifecycle_free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let lifecycle_parent = proc_mgr.spawn(
+        "lifecycle_parent",
+        crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+        crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+        0,
+        None,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let mut lifecycle_ok = false;
+    if let Ok(parent_pid) = lifecycle_parent {
+        let data_address = 0x0000_0000_0050_0000;
+        let status_address = data_address + 8;
+        let source_frame = pmm.allocate_frame();
+        if let (Some(frame), Some(parent)) = (source_frame, proc_mgr.get_process_mut(parent_pid)) {
+            unsafe {
+                let source = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(frame) as *mut u8;
+                core::ptr::write_bytes(source, 0, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
+                core::ptr::write_volatile(source, 0x5A);
+            }
+            if parent
+                .address_space
+                .map_page(data_address, frame, PageFlags::USER_DATA, pmm)
+                .is_ok()
+                && parent.resources.record_user_frame(frame)
+            {
+                let free_before_fork = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+                proc_mgr.current_pid = parent_pid;
+                let child_raw = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_FORK, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+                if child_raw != u64::MAX {
+                    let child_pid = child_raw as usize;
+                    let parent_process = proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid).copied();
+                    let child_process = proc_mgr.get_process(child_pid).copied();
+                    let (cow_shared, cow_split) = match (parent_process, child_process) {
+                        (Some(parent), Some(child)) => {
+                            let parent_frame = parent.address_space.translate_user(data_address);
+                            let child_frame = child.address_space.translate_user(data_address);
+                            let distinct_stacks = parent.kstack_top != 0
+                                && child.kstack_top != 0
+                                && parent.kstack_phys != child.kstack_phys
+                                && parent.address_space.pml4_phys != child.address_space.pml4_phys;
+                            // Copy-on-write: fork sonras─▒ iki s├╝re├ğ de ayn─▒
+                            // fiziksel ├ğer├ğeveyi payla┼ş─▒r, iki tarafta da PTE
+                            // salt-okunur + PAGE_COW olur ve i├ğerik korunur.
+                            let shared = match (parent_frame, child_frame) {
+                                (Some(parent_frame), Some(child_frame)) => {
+                                    let parent_byte = unsafe {
+                                        core::ptr::read_volatile(crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(
+                                            parent_frame,
+                                        )
+                                            as *const u8)
+                                    };
+                                    let child_byte = unsafe {
+                                        core::ptr::read_volatile(crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(
+                                            child_frame,
+                                        )
+                                            as *const u8)
+                                    };
+                                    let parent_entry =
+                                        parent.address_space.read_entry(data_address).unwrap_or(0);
+                                    let child_entry =
+                                        child.address_space.read_entry(data_address).unwrap_or(0);
+                                    parent_frame == child_frame
+                                        && parent_byte == 0x5A
+                                        && child_byte == 0x5A
+                                        && parent_entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE == 0
+                                        && child_entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE == 0
+                                        && parent_entry & PAGE_COW != 0
+                                        && child_entry & PAGE_COW != 0
+                                        && crate::mm::vmm::is_frame_shared(parent_frame)
+                                        && distinct_stacks
+                                }
+                                _ => false,
+                            };
+                            // ├çocu─şun ilk yazmas─▒ kendi ├Âzel kopyas─▒n─▒ ├╝retmeli,
+                            // ebeveynin sayfas─▒ de─şi┼şmemeli.
+                            let split = match (parent_frame, child_frame) {
+                                (Some(shared_frame), Some(_)) => {
+                                    let child_before =
+                                        child.address_space.translate_user(data_address);
+                                    let entry_before =
+                                        child.address_space.read_entry(data_address).unwrap_or(0);
+                                    let handled =
+                                        proc_mgr.handle_cow_fault(child.pid, data_address, pmm);
+                                    let child_after = proc_mgr
+                                        .get_process(child.pid)
+                                        .and_then(|c| c.address_space.translate_user(data_address));
+                                    let parent_after = proc_mgr
+                                        .get_process(parent_pid)
+                                        .and_then(|p| p.address_space.translate_user(data_address));
+                                    let ok = handled
+                                        && child_after
+                                            .is_some_and(|private| private != shared_frame)
+                                        && parent_after == Some(shared_frame)
+                                        && child_before == Some(shared_frame);
+                                    if !ok {
+                                        crate::serial_print("\n[PROC-TEST] split: handled=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_dec(handled as u64);
+                                        crate::serial_print(" shared=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_hex(shared_frame);
+                                        crate::serial_print(" child_before=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_hex(child_before.unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
+                                        crate::serial_print(" entry=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_hex(entry_before);
+                                        crate::serial_print(" child_after=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_hex(child_after.unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
+                                        crate::serial_print(" parent_after=");
+                                        crate::serial::print_hex(parent_after.unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
+                                        crate::serial_print("\n");
+                                    }
+                                    ok
+                                }
+                                _ => false,
+                            };
+                            (shared, split)
+                        }
+                        _ => (false, false),
+                    };
+
+                    // `SYS_WAIT` status i┼şaret├ğisini yazmadan ├Ânce
+                    // `uaccess::validate_user_range(.., write = true)` do─şrular;
+                    // fork sonras─▒ sayfa `PAGE_COW` + salt-okunur oldu─şundan bu
+                    // yazma reddedilir. Ger├ğek kullan─▒c─▒ program─▒ ilk yazmas─▒nda
+                    // #PF al─▒p COW kopyas─▒na ge├ğer; burada o ad─▒m─▒ sim├╝le ediyoruz.
+                    let status_cow = proc_mgr.handle_cow_fault(parent_pid, status_address, pmm);
+                    if !status_cow {
+                        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] status COW cozulemedi\n");
+                    }
+                    let pending_wait = syscall_dispatch(
+                        sys_nos::SYS_WAIT,
+                        child_pid as u64,
+                        status_address,
+                        0,
+                        proc_mgr,
+                        pmm,
+                    );
+                    let parent_blocked = pending_wait == 0
+                        && proc_mgr
+                            .get_process(parent_pid)
+                            .is_some_and(|parent| parent.state == ProcessState::Blocked);
+                    if !parent_blocked {
+                        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] wait1 ret=");
+                        crate::serial::print_hex(pending_wait);
+                        crate::serial_print(" status_addr=");
+                        crate::serial::print_hex(status_address);
+                        crate::serial_print(" entry=");
+                        crate::serial::print_hex(
+                            proc_mgr
+                                .get_process(parent_pid)
+                                .and_then(|p| p.address_space.read_entry(status_address))
+                                .unwrap_or(0),
+                        );
+                        crate::serial_print(" state=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(
+                            proc_mgr
+                                .get_process(parent_pid)
+                                .map(|p| match p.state {
+                                    ProcessState::Ready => 1,
+                                    ProcessState::Running => 2,
+                                    ProcessState::Blocked => 3,
+                                    ProcessState::Suspended => 6,
+                                    ProcessState::Terminated => 4,
+                                    ProcessState::Unused => 5,
+                                })
+                                .unwrap_or(9),
+                        );
+                        crate::serial_print("\n");
+                    }
+                    proc_mgr.current_pid = child_pid;
+                    let child_exit = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_EXIT, 42, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+                    let waiter_woken = child_exit == 0
+                        && proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid).is_some_and(|parent| {
+                            parent.state == ProcessState::Ready
+                                && parent.context.rax == child_pid as u64
+                        });
+                    proc_mgr.current_pid = parent_pid;
+                    let wait_result = syscall_dispatch(
+                        sys_nos::SYS_WAIT,
+                        child_pid as u64,
+                        status_address,
+                        0,
+                        proc_mgr,
+                        pmm,
+                    );
+                    let status = proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid).is_some_and(|parent| {
+                        parent
+                            .address_space
+                            .translate_user(status_address)
+                            .is_some_and(|physical| unsafe {
+                                core::ptr::read_volatile(
+                                    crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(physical) as *const i32
+                                ) == 42
+                            })
+                    });
+                    let reaped_ret = wait_result == child_pid as u64;
+                    let reaped_gone = proc_mgr.get_process(child_pid).is_none();
+                    let reaped_free = pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before_fork;
+                    if !(reaped_ret && reaped_gone && reaped_free) {
+                        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] reap: ret=");
+                        crate::serial::print_hex(wait_result);
+                        crate::serial_print(" pid=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(child_pid as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" gone=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(reaped_gone as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" free_before=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(free_before_fork);
+                        crate::serial_print(" free_now=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(pmm.free_memory_bytes);
+                        crate::serial_print("\n");
+                    }
+                    let child_reaped = reaped_ret && reaped_gone && reaped_free;
+
+                    let root_before_exec = proc_mgr
+                        .get_process(parent_pid)
+                        .map(|parent| parent.address_space.pml4_phys)
+                        .unwrap_or(0);
+                    let failed_exec = syscall_dispatch(
+                        sys_nos::SYS_EXEC,
+                        0x0000_0000_6000_0000,
+                        16,
+                        0,
+                        proc_mgr,
+                        pmm,
+                    ) == u64::MAX
+                        && proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid).is_some_and(|parent| {
+                            parent.address_space.pml4_phys == root_before_exec
+                        });
+
+                    let raw_image = [0x31, 0xFF, 0xB8, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0xCD, 0x80];
+                    if let Some(parent) = proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid) {
+                        if let Some(physical) = parent.address_space.translate_user(data_address) {
+                            unsafe {
+                                let destination = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(physical) as *mut u8;
+                                core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(
+                                    raw_image.as_ptr(),
+                                    destination.add(32),
+                                    raw_image.len(),
+                                );
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    let exec_result = syscall_dispatch(
+                        sys_nos::SYS_EXEC,
+                        data_address + 32,
+                        raw_image.len() as u64,
+                        0,
+                        proc_mgr,
+                        pmm,
+                    );
+                    let exec_ok = exec_result == 0
+                        && proc_mgr.get_process(parent_pid).is_some_and(|parent| {
+                            parent.address_space.pml4_phys != root_before_exec
+                                && parent.context.rip == crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE
+                                && parent
+                                    .address_space
+                                    .translate_user(crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16)
+                                    .is_some()
+                                && parent.kstack_top != 0
+                        });
+                    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+                    let parent_terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(parent_pid, 0, pmm);
+                    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+                    if !(cow_shared
+                        && cow_split
+                        && parent_blocked
+                        && waiter_woken
+                        && status
+                        && child_reaped
+                        && failed_exec
+                        && exec_ok)
+                    {
+                        crate::serial_print("[PROC-TEST] fork/exec detay: cow_shared=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(cow_shared as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" cow_split=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(cow_split as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" blocked=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(parent_blocked as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" woken=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(waiter_woken as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" status=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(status as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" reaped=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(child_reaped as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" bad_exec=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(failed_exec as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print(" exec=");
+                        crate::serial::print_dec(exec_ok as u64);
+                        crate::serial_print("\n");
+                    }
+                    lifecycle_ok = cow_shared
+                        && cow_split
+                        && parent_blocked
+                        && waiter_woken
+                        && status
+                        && child_reaped
+                        && failed_exec
+                        && exec_ok
+                        && parent_terminated
+                        && pmm.free_memory_bytes == lifecycle_free_before;
+                }
+            } else if let Some(frame) = source_frame {
+                pmm.free_frame(frame);
+            }
+        } else if let Some(frame) = source_frame {
+            pmm.free_frame(frame);
+        }
+        if !lifecycle_ok {
+            proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+            let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(parent_pid, -1, pmm);
+            proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if sched_ok && rr_ok && lifecycle_ok {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PROC-TEST] 6/7. Oncelik/round-robin ve fork/exec/wait yasam dongusu: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PROC-TEST] Zamanlayici veya fork/exec/wait yasam dongusu: BASARISIZ\n",
+        );
+        false
+    }
+}
+
+/// Ring 3 adres uzay─▒ izolasyonu ve kernel mapping kopyalama testi.
+/// Yeni bir s├╝re├ğ olu┼şturur, kernel mapping'lerin kopyaland─▒─ş─▒n─▒ do─şrular.
+pub fn test_ring3_isolation(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] --- Ring 3 ─░zolasyon ve Kernel Mapping Testi ---\n");
+
+    let initial_free = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+
+    // 1. Yeni bir kullan─▒c─▒ s├╝reci olu┼ştur
+    let pid = match proc_mgr.spawn("ring3_test", 0x1000, 0x7000_2000, 100, None, pmm) {
+        Ok(id) => id,
+        Err(e) => {
+            crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturma hatas─▒: ");
+            crate::serial_print(e);
+            crate::serial_print("\n");
+            return false;
+        }
+    };
+    crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] 1. S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturuldu (PID ");
+    crate::serial::print_dec(pid as u64);
+    crate::serial_print("): BA┼ŞARILI\n");
+
+    // 2. Kernel mapping kopyalanmas─▒n─▒ ve supervisor-only izinlerini do─şrula.
+    if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process(pid) {
+        let aspace = p.address_space;
+        let kernel_cr3 = AddressSpace::current().pml4_phys;
+        if aspace.pml4_phys == kernel_cr3 || p.context.cr3 != aspace.pml4_phys {
+            crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] Ayr─▒ CR3/adres uzay─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+            let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        // Kernel mapping'ler PML4 ├╝st yar─▒s─▒nda (indeks 256-511) olmal─▒
+        unsafe {
+            let pml4 = &*crate::mm::vmm::AddressSpace::table_ptr(aspace.pml4_phys);
+            let mut kernel_entries = 0usize;
+            let mut user_visible_kernel_entries = 0usize;
+            for entry in pml4.entries[256..512].iter() {
+                if (entry & crate::mm::vmm::PageFlags::PRESENT) != 0 {
+                    kernel_entries += 1;
+                    if (entry & crate::mm::vmm::PageFlags::USER) != 0 {
+                        user_visible_kernel_entries += 1;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            if kernel_entries > 0 && user_visible_kernel_entries == 0 {
+                crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] 2. Kernel mapping kopyaland─▒ (");
+                crate::serial::print_dec(kernel_entries as u64);
+                crate::serial_print(" giri┼ş), USER biti yok: BA┼ŞARILI\n");
+                crate::serial_print("[P1] CR3 ayri ve kernel mapping Ring3'e kapali: BASARILI\n");
+            } else {
+                crate::serial_print(
+                    "[RING3-TEST] Kernel mapping USER eri┼şimine a├ğ─▒k veya yok: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n",
+                );
+                let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    // 3. S├╝reci temizle ve kaynak iadesini kontrol et
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+    if !terminated {
+        crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] 3. S├╝re├ğ sonland─▒rma: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    let free_after = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    if free_after != initial_free {
+        crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] 3. Kaynak temizleme s─▒z─▒nt─▒s─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    crate::serial_print("[RING3-TEST] 3. Kaynak temizleme s─▒z─▒nt─▒s─▒z: BA┼ŞARILI\n");
+    crate::serial_print("[P1] process kaynak temizligi: BASARILI\n");
+    true
+}
+
+/// Syscall kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şaret├ği (pointer) do─şrulamalar─▒n─▒ tek noktada toplayan test.
+///
+/// Kapsam: `SYS_WRITE`, `SYS_WAIT`, `SYS_EXEC`, `SYS_MMAP`, `SYS_MUNMAP` ve
+/// `SYS_MEMINFO` yollar─▒ndaki t├╝m kullan─▒c─▒ adresleri `translate_user` ile
+/// do─şrulan─▒r. Bu test; NULL, hizas─▒z, yar─▒ e┼şlenmi┼ş, ├ğekirdek yar─▒ adresi ve
+/// s─▒n─▒r a┼şan uzunluklar─▒n reddedildi─şini, ge├ğerli ├ğa─şr─▒lar─▒n ise ├ğal─▒┼şt─▒─ş─▒n─▒
+/// kan─▒tlar. Ayr─▒ca hatal─▒ ├ğa─şr─▒lar─▒n PMM'de ├ğer├ğeve s─▒zd─▒rmad─▒─ş─▒n─▒ denetler.
+pub fn test_syscall_pointer_validation(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] --- Syscall kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şaret├ği do─şrulama testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+
+    let Some(pid) = proc_mgr
+        .spawn(
+            "ptr_test",
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+        .ok()
+    else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    };
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // Test tamponu: iki sayfa, kullanici veri bayraklariyla eslenir.
+    let buffer_address: u64 = 0x0000_0000_0060_0000;
+    let Some(buffer_frame) = pmm.allocate_frame() else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] ├çer├ğeve tahsisi: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, -1, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        return false;
+    };
+    let mapped = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).is_some_and(|process| {
+        process
+            .address_space
+            .map_page(buffer_address, buffer_frame, PageFlags::USER_DATA, pmm)
+            .is_ok()
+            && process.resources.record_user_frame(buffer_frame)
+    });
+    if !mapped {
+        pmm.free_frame(buffer_frame);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, -1, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] Tampon e┼şlenemedi: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // Ikinci sayfa: salt-okunur (WRITABLE yok) kullanici eslemesi. Yazma yollari
+    // bu sayfayi reddetmeli; okuma yollari kabul etmeli.
+    let readonly_address: u64 = buffer_address + PAGE_SIZE;
+    let readonly_mapped = pmm.allocate_frame().is_some_and(|frame| {
+        let ok = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).is_some_and(|process| {
+            process
+                .address_space
+                .map_page(readonly_address, frame, PageFlags::USER_CODE, pmm)
+                .is_ok()
+                && process.resources.record_user_frame(frame)
+        });
+        if !ok {
+            pmm.free_frame(frame);
+        }
+        ok
+    });
+    if !readonly_mapped {
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, -1, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] Salt-okunur sayfa eslenemedi: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    }
+    // Tamponu taninabilir bir desenle doldur (WRITE geri okumasini dogrulamak icin).
+    let filled = proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|process| {
+        let physical = process.address_space.translate_user(buffer_address);
+        physical.is_some_and(|phys| unsafe {
+            let base = crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(phys) as *mut u8;
+            for offset in 0..64usize {
+                core::ptr::write_volatile(base.add(offset), b'A');
+            }
+            true
+        })
+    });
+    if !filled {
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] Tampon doldurulamadi: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+    }
+
+    // --- Reddedilmesi gereken i┼şaret├ğiler -------------------------------------
+    // Eslenmemis adres: iki sayfa sonrasi (tampon + salt-okunur sayfa dolu).
+    let unmapped_address: u64 = buffer_address + 2 * PAGE_SIZE;
+
+    // (a) Cekirdek yarisi isaretcileri.
+    //   - Cekirdek metni (islev isaretcisi).
+    let kernel_address = test_syscall_pointer_validation as *const () as u64;
+    let kernel_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, kernel_address, 8, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - Kanonik cekirdek tabani (HHDM basi): USER yarinin ustunde.
+    let kernel_base_write = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        0xFFFF_8000_0000_0000u64,
+        8,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    //   - Kanonik olmayan adres: kullanici yarisi sinirinin hemen ustu.
+    let noncanonical_write = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        uaccess::USER_ADDRESS_END,
+        8,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+
+    // (b) Adres/uzunluk aritmetigi sinirlari.
+    //   - NULL (uzunluk > 0).
+    let null_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, 0, 64, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - Tasma: address + len u64'u asar.
+    let overflow_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, u64::MAX - 3, 8, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - Kullanici yarisi sinirinin son baytlari: aralik gecerli ama eslenmemis.
+    let user_end_write = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        uaccess::USER_ADDRESS_END - 8,
+        8,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+
+    // (c) Sayfa/hizalama sinirlari.
+    //   - Eslenmemis sayfaya yazma.
+    let unmapped_write =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, unmapped_address, 8, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - Yari eslenmis aralik: ilk 8 bayt okunabilir sayfada, kalan 8 bayt
+    //     eslenmemis sayfaya tasiyor; tum cagri reddedilmeli.
+    let straddle_write = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        readonly_address + PAGE_SIZE - 8,
+        16,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+
+    // (d) Uzunluk sinirlari: 0 etkisiz, 1024 kabul, 1025 reddedilir.
+    let empty_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, buffer_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let max_len_write =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, buffer_address, 1024, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let oversized_write =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, buffer_address, 1025, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+
+    // (e) Diger syscall yollari.
+    //   - SYS_WAIT: arg2 (durum isaretcisi) salt-okunur / eslenmemis.
+    let wait_ro_status = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WAIT, 0, readonly_address, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let wait_bad_status =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WAIT, 0, unmapped_address, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - SYS_MEMINFO: salt-okunur / eslenmemis rapor adresi.
+    let meminfo_ro = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, readonly_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let meminfo_bad = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, unmapped_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - SYS_EXEC: arg1 NULL/eslenmemis, arg2 sifir veya asiri buyuk.
+    let exec_null = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_EXEC, 0, 16, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let exec_unmapped = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_EXEC, unmapped_address, 16, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let exec_empty = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_EXEC, buffer_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let exec_oversized = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_EXEC,
+        buffer_address,
+        MAX_EXEC_IMAGE_SIZE as u64 + 1,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    //   - SYS_OPEN: NULL ve eslenmemis yol.
+    let open_null = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let open_unmapped = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, unmapped_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - SYS_MMAP: sifir/asiri uzunluk reddedilir (0 doner).
+    let mmap_zero = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let mmap_oversized =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, 64 * 1024 * 1024 + 1, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    //   - SYS_MUNMAP: NULL adres ve sifir uzunluk reddedilir.
+    let munmap_null = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, 0, PAGE_SIZE, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let munmap_zero_len =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, buffer_address, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+
+    // (f) Kopyalama katmani sozlesmesi (kutuphane seviyesi, deterministik):
+    //     salt-okunur sayfadan okuma serbest, yazma reddedilir.
+    let (ro_read_ok, ro_write_rejected) = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|process| {
+            let read_ok =
+                uaccess::copy_from_user(&process.address_space, readonly_address, 8).is_ok();
+            let write_err =
+                uaccess::copy_to_user(&process.address_space, readonly_address, &[0u8; 8]);
+            (
+                read_ok,
+                matches!(write_err, Err(uaccess::UserCopyError::NotWritable)),
+            )
+        })
+        .unwrap_or((false, false));
+
+    let rejected = null_write == 0
+        && overflow_write == 0
+        && user_end_write == 0
+        && kernel_write == 0
+        && kernel_base_write == 0
+        && noncanonical_write == 0
+        && unmapped_write == 0
+        && straddle_write == 0
+        && empty_write == 0
+        && max_len_write == 1024
+        && oversized_write == 0
+        && wait_ro_status == u64::MAX
+        && wait_bad_status == u64::MAX
+        && meminfo_ro == u64::MAX
+        && meminfo_bad == u64::MAX
+        && exec_null == u64::MAX
+        && exec_unmapped == u64::MAX
+        && exec_empty == u64::MAX
+        && exec_oversized == u64::MAX
+        && open_null == u64::MAX
+        && open_unmapped == u64::MAX
+        && mmap_zero == 0
+        && mmap_oversized == 0
+        && munmap_null == u64::MAX
+        && munmap_zero_len == u64::MAX
+        && ro_read_ok
+        && ro_write_rejected;
+
+    // --- Kabul edilmesi gereken cagrilar -------------------------------------
+    let valid_write = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, buffer_address, 8, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let write_ok = valid_write == 8;
+    // Tamponun son baytina kadar (sayfa sinirinin hemen altinda) yazma.
+    let edge_write = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        buffer_address + PAGE_SIZE - 8,
+        8,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let edge_ok = edge_write == 8;
+    // Tek bayt: tamponun ilk ve son bayti.
+    let first_byte = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, buffer_address, 1, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let last_byte = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_WRITE,
+        buffer_address + PAGE_SIZE - 1,
+        1,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let byte_edges_ok = first_byte == 1 && last_byte == 1;
+    // SYS_WRITE kullan─▒c─▒ belle─şini okur: salt-okunur sayfa da ge├ğerli bir
+    // kaynak olarak kabul edilir (korunan y├Ân `copy_to_user` iledir).
+    let readonly_read = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, readonly_address, 8, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let readonly_read_ok = readonly_read == 8;
+    // Eslenmemis bir mmap b├Âlgesi ├╝zerinde munmap tekrar reddedilmeli.
+    let unmap_unmapped = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP,
+        unmapped_address,
+        PAGE_SIZE,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let double_unmap_rejected = unmap_unmapped == u64::MAX;
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    // Sizinti denetimi: surec sonlandirilip kaynaklari iade edildikten sonra
+    // PMM tekrar baslangic degerine donmeli.
+    let no_leak = pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before;
+    let reclaimed = no_leak && terminated;
+
+    let ok = filled
+        && rejected
+        && write_ok
+        && edge_ok
+        && byte_edges_ok
+        && ro_read_ok
+        && double_unmap_rejected
+        && readonly_read_ok
+        && reclaimed;
+    if ok {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PTR-TEST] NULL/hizasiz/yari-eslenmis/cekirdek/salt-okunur isaretcileri ve sinir uzunluklari reddedildi, gecerli cagrilar calisti, sizinti yok: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PTR-TEST] Syscall isaretci dogrulamasi: BASARISIZ (");
+        crate::serial_print(if filled { "fill " } else { "fill! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if rejected { "reject " } else { "reject! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if write_ok { "write " } else { "write! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if edge_ok { "edge " } else { "edge! " });
