+        crate::serial_print(if byte_edges_ok { "bytes " } else { "bytes! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if ro_read_ok { "ro-read " } else { "ro-read! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if readonly_read_ok {
+            "ro-syscall "
+        } else {
+            "ro-syscall! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if double_unmap_rejected {
+            "2munmap "
+        } else {
+            "2munmap! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "noleak " } else { "noleak! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if reclaimed { "reclaim" } else { "reclaim!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+    }
+    ok
+}
+
+/// Seccomp-tarz─▒ syscall izin maskesi (`SYS_SECCOMP` / `Process::syscall_mask`)
+/// do─şrulama testi. Filtrelemenin daralt─▒c─▒ (AND-only) oldu─şunu, engellenen
+/// ├ğa─şr─▒lar─▒n `u64::MAX` d├Ând├╝rd├╝─ş├╝n├╝ ve `SYS_EXIT`'in her zaman serbest
+/// kald─▒─ş─▒n─▒ ger├ğek dispatch yoluyla s─▒nar.
+pub fn test_syscall_filter(proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[SECCOMP-TEST] --- Syscall izin maskesi (seccomp) testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    let Some(pid) = proc_mgr
+        .spawn(
+            "seccomp_test",
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+        .ok()
+    else {
+        crate::serial_print("[SECCOMP-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    };
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // Maske: GETPID(2) + WRITE(5) + SECCOMP(28) serbest; MEMINFO(14),
+    // OPEN(15), EXIT(1) maskede yok (EXIT yine de muaf).
+    let mask_a = (1u64 << sys_nos::SYS_GETPID)
+        | (1u64 << sys_nos::SYS_WRITE)
+        | (1u64 << sys_nos::SYS_SECCOMP);
+
+    // 1. Filtresiz s├╝re├ğ: GETPID pid d├Ând├╝r├╝r, MEMINFO checksum ├╝retir.
+    let unfiltered_pid = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_GETPID, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let unfiltered_meminfo = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+
+    // 2. Maske kur: yaln─▒zca daraltma kabul edilir.
+    let set_ok = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_SECCOMP, mask_a, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0;
+    let stored = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|process| process.syscall_mask)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+
+    // 3. ─░zinli ├ğa─şr─▒lar ├ğal─▒┼ş─▒r, izinsiz olanlar u64::MAX d├Âner.
+    let getpid_ok = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_GETPID, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == pid as u64;
+    let write_allowed = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_WRITE, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0;
+    let meminfo_blocked =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+    let open_blocked = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 4. Geni┼şletme giri┼şimi yok say─▒l─▒r: maskeye u64::MAX verilse bile
+    //    AND semanti─şi eski maskeyi korur.
+    let _ = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_SECCOMP, u64::MAX, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let still_blocked = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 5. ─░kinci daraltma: GETPID biti de kapat─▒l─▒r.
+    let mask_b = mask_a & !(1u64 << sys_nos::SYS_GETPID);
+    let _ = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_SECCOMP, mask_b, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let getpid_now_blocked =
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_GETPID, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 6. SYS_EXIT maskede olmasa bile her zaman serbesttir (sonland─▒rma
+    //    hakk─▒). Bu ├ğa─şr─▒ s├╝reci Terminated yapar; temizlik a┼şa─ş─▒da.
+    let exit_allowed = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_EXIT, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0;
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+
+    let ok = unfiltered_pid == pid as u64
+        && unfiltered_meminfo != u64::MAX
+        && set_ok
+        && stored == mask_a
+        && getpid_ok
+        && write_allowed
+        && meminfo_blocked
+        && open_blocked
+        && still_blocked
+        && getpid_now_blocked
+        && exit_allowed;
+    crate::serial_print(if ok {
+        "[SECCOMP-TEST] daraltma/AND-only/engel/suzinti-yok/EXIT-muaf: BASARILI\n"
+    } else {
+        "[SECCOMP-TEST] Syscall izin maskesi: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n"
+    });
+    ok
+}
+
+/// Kullan─▒c─▒ heap/`brk`, `mmap`/`munmap`, ASLR ve copy-on-write do─şrulama testi.
+///
+/// S─▒f─▒rlam─▒┼ş ├ğer├ğevelerle ├ğal─▒┼ş─▒r (ger├ğek kullan─▒c─▒ y─▒─ş─▒n─▒/kodu de─şil); ama├ğ
+/// bellek semanti─şini ger├ğek sayfa tablolar─▒ ve PMM ├╝zerinde do─şrulamakt─▒r.
+pub fn test_user_memory(proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[USER-MEM] --- Kullan─▒c─▒ heap/mmap, ASLR ve COW do─şrulama testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+
+    let Some(pid) = proc_mgr
+        .spawn(
+            "usermem",
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+        .ok()
+    else {
+        crate::serial_print("[USER-MEM] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    };
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // 1. brk: heap b├╝y├╝t├╝l├╝r (tembel e┼şleme, ├ğer├ğeve ayr─▒lmaz) ve ilk yazma
+    //    ile her sayfa s─▒f─▒r gelmi┼ş olarak ger├ğekle┼ştirilir.
+    let free_before_heap = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let heap_end = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, 3 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    // Lazy PTE'ler payla┼ş─▒lan s─▒f─▒r sayfas─▒n─▒ g├Âsterir: en fazla bir kez
+    // (ilk tembel e┼şlemede) tek bir s─▒f─▒r ├ğer├ğevesi ayr─▒l─▒r.
+    let lazy_heap_ok = pmm.free_memory_bytes + PAGE_SIZE >= free_before_heap
+        && proc_mgr.lazy_pages(pid) == 3
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            (0..3).all(|i| {
+                let address = p.heap_address(i as u64 * PAGE_SIZE);
+                p.address_space.read_entry(address).is_some_and(|entry| {
+                    entry & PAGE_LAZY != 0
+                        && entry & PageFlags::PRESENT != 0
+                        && entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE == 0
+                        && entry & PageFlags::NO_EXECUTE != 0
+                })
+            })
+        });
+    // ─░lk yazma: tembel sayfalar ├Ânce s─▒f─▒rlanm─▒┼ş ├Âzel ├ğer├ğevelere
+    // d├Ân├╝┼şt├╝r├╝l├╝r (Ring 3 yolu `#PF` ÔåÆ `handle_lazy_fault`), sonra yaz─▒l─▒r.
+    for i in 0..3u64 {
+        let _ =
+            proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + i * PAGE_SIZE, pmm);
+        if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process(pid) {
+            let _ = write_user_u64(p, p.heap_address(i * PAGE_SIZE), 0x1122_3344 + i);
+        }
+    }
+    let heap_ok = heap_end == crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + 3 * PAGE_SIZE
+        && lazy_heap_ok
+        && (0..3u64).all(|i| {
+            proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+                let address = p.heap_address(i * PAGE_SIZE);
+                p.address_space
+                    .translate_user(address)
+                    .and_then(|phys| {
+                        p.address_space
+                            .read_entry(address)
+                            .map(|entry| (phys, entry))
+                    })
+                    .is_some_and(|(phys, entry)| {
+                        entry & PageFlags::WRITABLE != 0
+                            && entry & PageFlags::NO_EXECUTE != 0
+                            && entry & PAGE_LAZY == 0
+                            && unsafe {
+                                core::ptr::read_volatile(
+                                    crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(phys) as *const u64
+                                ) == 0x1122_3344 + i
+                            }
+                    })
+            })
+        });
+    // 1b. Heap u├ğlar─▒nda yazma/okuma ├ğal─▒┼ş─▒r (in bounds).
+    let heap_write_ok = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .is_some_and(|p| write_user_u64(p, p.heap_address(0), 0xDEAD_BEEF_CAFE_F00D));
+    // 1c. Hemen ├╝st├╝ndeki e┼şlenmemi┼ş sayfaya ├ğekirdek eri┼şimi reddedilir.
+    let heap_bounds_ok = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .is_some_and(|p| !write_user_u64(p, p.heap_address(3 * PAGE_SIZE), 1));
+
+    // 2. brk k├╝├ğ├╝ltme: yaln─▒z kalan son sayfa serbest b─▒rak─▒l─▒r.
+    let free_before_shrink = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let shrunk = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, 2 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let shrink_ok = shrunk == crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + 2 * PAGE_SIZE
+        && pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before_shrink + PAGE_SIZE
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            p.address_space
+                .translate(p.heap_address(2 * PAGE_SIZE))
+                .is_none()
+        });
+
+    // 3. mmap/munmap: b├Âlge tembel e┼şlenir; ilk yazma sayfay─▒ ger├ğekle┼ştirir.
+    //    Sayfa tablosu ├ğer├ğeveleri kaynak defterine girmedi─şi i├ğin tembellik
+    //    `user_frame_count` ├╝zerinden do─şrulan─▒r.
+    let user_frames_before_mmap = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|p| p.resources.user_frame_count)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let region = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, 2 * 4096, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let lazy_mmap_ok = region != 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.resources.user_frame_count == user_frames_before_mmap);
+    // Tembel sayfay─▒ kullan─▒c─▒ CR3'├╝ olmadan ├ğekirdek yolundan ger├ğekle┼ştir
+    // (Ring 3 yazma yolu `#PF` ÔåÆ `handle_lazy_fault` iken bu e┼şde─şer ├ğa─şr─▒d─▒r).
+    if region != 0 {
+        let _ = proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, region, pmm);
+    }
+    let region_pages = lazy_mmap_ok
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            region == p.mmap_base
+                && region.is_multiple_of(PAGE_SIZE)
+                && (crate::mm::loader::ASLR_MMAP_MIN..crate::mm::loader::ASLR_MMAP_MAX)
+                    .contains(&region)
+                && p.address_space
+                    .translate_user(region)
+                    .is_some_and(|phys| unsafe {
+                        core::ptr::read_volatile(crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(phys) as *const u64)
+                            == 0
+                    })
+        });
+    let second = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let second_ok = second == region + 2 * PAGE_SIZE;
+    let free_before_unmap = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let unmap = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, region, 2 * 4096, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let free_after_unmap = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    // `region` iki sayfa, yaln─▒z ilk sayfa ger├ğekle┼ştirildi: munmap tam 1 sayfa
+    // iade eder; kullan─▒lmayan tembel sayfa fiziksel bellek t├╝ketmedi.
+    let unmap_ok = unmap == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.address_space.translate(region).is_none())
+        && free_after_unmap == free_before_unmap + PAGE_SIZE;
+    let unmap_twice = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, region, 2 * 4096, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let mmap_ok = region_pages && second_ok && unmap_ok && unmap_twice == u64::MAX;
+    // `second` tembel sayfas─▒n─▒ da ger├ğekle┼ştir: geni┼şletilmi┼ş MEMINFO'daki
+    // kullan─▒c─▒ ├ğer├ğevesi say─▒s─▒ heap (2) + bu sayfa (1) = 3 olur.
+    if second != 0 {
+        let _ = proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, second, pmm);
+    }
+
+    // 4. MEMINFO syscall'u (R10 yolu) ├Âzeti do─şru raporlar.
+    let heap_address = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|p| p.heap_address(0))
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let report_addr = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .and_then(|p| {
+            let address = p.heap_address(0);
+            p.address_space.translate_user(address).map(|_| address)
+        })
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let meminfo = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MEMINFO, report_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let meminfo_ok = report_addr != 0
+        && meminfo == 0
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            let heap = p
+                .address_space
+                .translate_user(report_addr)
+                .map(|phys| unsafe {
+                    core::ptr::read_volatile(crate::drivers::phys_to_virt(phys) as *const u64)
+                })
+                .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
+            // MEMINFO raporu heap ucunu (2 sayfa) ilk alan olarak d├Âner.
+            heap == 2 * PAGE_SIZE && heap_address != 0
+        });
+
+    // 4b. Geni┼şletilmi┼ş MEMINFO: u64 x 12 rapor; kullan─▒c─▒ ├ğer├ğevesi ve sistem
+    //     toplam/bo┼ş bellek alanlar─▒ makul olmal─▒.
+    let meminfo_ext_ok = report_addr != 0
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            uaccess::copy_from_user(&p.address_space, report_addr, 96)
+                .map(|bytes| {
+                    let value = |index: usize| {
+                        let mut raw = [0u8; 8];
+                        raw.copy_from_slice(&bytes[index * 8..index * 8 + 8]);
+                        u64::from_le_bytes(raw)
+                    };
+                    let user_frames = p.resources.user_frame_count as u64;
+                    value(0) == p.heap_brk
+                        && value(4) == user_frames
+                        && user_frames >= 3
+                        && value(7) > 0
+                        && value(8) > 0
+                        && value(7) > value(8)
+                        && value(9) == user_frames
+                        && value(10) > 0
+                })
+                .unwrap_or(false)
+        });
+
+    // 4c. Sayfa payla┼ş─▒m─▒ optimizasyonu: `FILE_SHARED` mmap b├Âlgesini iki
+    //     s├╝re├ğ e┼şler; ayn─▒ fiziksel ├ğer├ğeve payla┼ş─▒l─▒r, kopya yoktur.
+    //     ─░kinci s├╝re├ğ b├Âlgeyi b─▒rak─▒nca birinci s├╝recin e┼şlemesi bozulmaz.
+    let share_pid = proc_mgr
+        .spawn("share", 0x1000, 0x7000_0000, 0, None, pmm)
+        .ok();
+    let share_free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let mut shared_frame_matched = false;
+    if let (Some(reader), Some(writer)) = (Some(pid), share_pid) {
+        let flags = crate::mm::user_mem::MAP_SHARED_FILE | PageFlags::USER;
+        let reader_addr = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, PAGE_SIZE, flags, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = writer;
+        let writer_addr = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, PAGE_SIZE, flags, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+        shared_frame_matched = reader_addr != 0
+            && writer_addr == reader_addr
+            && proc_mgr.get_process(reader).is_some_and(|p| {
+                let entry = p.address_space.read_entry(reader_addr).unwrap_or(0);
+                entry & crate::mm::user_mem::PAGE_SHARED != 0
+            })
+            && proc_mgr.get_process(writer).is_some_and(|p| {
+                let entry = p.address_space.read_entry(writer_addr).unwrap_or(0);
+                entry & crate::mm::user_mem::PAGE_SHARED != 0
+            })
+            && proc_mgr
+                .get_process(reader)
+                .zip(proc_mgr.get_process(writer))
+                .is_some_and(|(a, b)| {
+                    let pa = a
+                        .address_space
+                        .translate_user(reader_addr)
+                        .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
+                    let pb = b
+                        .address_space
+                        .translate_user(writer_addr)
+                        .unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
+                    pa != u64::MAX && pa == pb
+                });
+        // Yazar s├╝recin e┼şlemesini b─▒rak; okuyucunun e┼şlemesi korunur.
+        // Ard─▒ndan okuyucu da b─▒rak─▒r; ├ğer├ğeve son sahiple birlikte iade edilir.
+        if shared_frame_matched {
+            proc_mgr.current_pid = writer;
+            let unmapped = syscall_dispatch(
+                sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP,
+                writer_addr,
+                PAGE_SIZE,
+                0,
+                proc_mgr,
+                pmm,
+            );
+            proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+            shared_frame_matched = unmapped == 0
+                && proc_mgr
+                    .get_process(reader)
+                    .is_some_and(|p| p.address_space.translate_user(reader_addr).is_some());
+            let released = syscall_dispatch(
+                sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP,
+                reader_addr,
+                PAGE_SIZE,
+                0,
+                proc_mgr,
+                pmm,
+            );
+            shared_frame_matched = shared_frame_matched && released == 0;
+        }
+        // Payla┼ş─▒ml─▒ sayfa (1) d─▒┼ş─▒nda ek fiziksel bellek kullan─▒lmamal─▒.
+        shared_frame_matched = shared_frame_matched
+            && share_free_before.saturating_sub(pmm.free_memory_bytes) <= PAGE_SIZE;
+        crate::mm::user_mem::release_shared_regions();
+    }
+
+    // 5. ASLR: iki s├╝re├ğ farkl─▒ mmap taban─▒ se├ğmeli (entropi testi).
+    let mut bases = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+    bases.push(proc_mgr.get_process(pid).map(|p| p.mmap_base).unwrap_or(0));
+    let mut aslr_pids = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+    for _ in 0..6u64 {
+        if let Ok(other) = proc_mgr.spawn("aslr", 0x1000, 0x7000_0000, 0, None, pmm) {
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process(other) {
+                bases.push(p.mmap_base);
+            }
+            aslr_pids.push(other);
+        }
+    }
+    let aslr_distinct = {
+        let mut seen = alloc::vec::Vec::new();
+        for base in &bases {
+            if !seen.contains(base) {
+                seen.push(*base);
+            }
+        }
+        seen.len() > 1 && bases.iter().all(|b| b % 0x20_0000 == 0)
+    };
+    // ASLR y─▒─ş─▒n─▒: rastgele ├╝st se├ğimi kullan─▒c─▒ y─▒─ş─▒n─▒ndan a┼şa─ş─▒, hizal─▒ olmal─▒.
+    let stack_tops: alloc::vec::Vec<u64> = (0..8)
+        .map(|_| crate::mm::loader::randomized_stack_top())
+        .collect();
+    let stack_ok = stack_tops.iter().all(|top| {
+        *top <= crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP
+            && *top > crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - crate::mm::loader::ASLR_STACK_MAX_SLIDE
+            && top % PAGE_SIZE == 0
+    }) && stack_tops.iter().any(|top| *top != stack_tops[0]);
+
+    for other in aslr_pids {
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(other, 0, pmm);
+    }
+    if let Some(writer) = share_pid {
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(writer, 0, pmm);
+    }
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let no_leak = pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before
+        && crate::mm::vmm::shared_frame_count() == 0
+        && terminated;
+
+    if heap_ok
+        && heap_write_ok
+        && heap_bounds_ok
+        && shrink_ok
+        && mmap_ok
+        && meminfo_ok
+        && meminfo_ext_ok
+        && shared_frame_matched
+        && aslr_distinct
+        && stack_ok
+        && no_leak
+    {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[USER-MEM] heap/mmap/ASLR: brk buyut+kucult, tembel sayfa, mmap/munmap, MEMINFO, sayfa paylasimi ve sizintisiz iade: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[USER-MEM] heap/mmap/ASLR testi: BASARISIZ (");
+        crate::serial_print(if heap_ok { "heap " } else { "heap! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if heap_write_ok { "hw " } else { "hw! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if heap_bounds_ok { "hb " } else { "hb! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if shrink_ok { "shrink " } else { "shrink! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if region_pages { "region " } else { "region! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if mmap_ok { "mmap " } else { "mmap! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if second_ok { "2nd " } else { "2nd! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if unmap_ok { "unmap " } else { "unmap! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if meminfo_ok { "meminfo " } else { "meminfo! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if meminfo_ext_ok {
+            "meminfo-ext "
+        } else {
+            "meminfo-ext! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if shared_frame_matched {
+            "shared "
+        } else {
+            "shared! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if aslr_distinct && stack_ok {
+            "aslr "
+        } else {
+            "aslr! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "leak-free" } else { "leak!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+        false
+    }
+}
+
+/// Dosya tan─▒t─▒c─▒s─▒ ya┼şam d├Âng├╝s├╝n├╝ do─şrular: a├ğma, okuma, konumland─▒rma,
+/// kapatma, `fork` devralma/refcount, `exec` s─▒f─▒rlama ve fd kotas─▒.
+///
+/// Kullan─▒c─▒ tamponu ger├ğek bir heap sayfas─▒d─▒r; yollar `uaccess` katman─▒ndan
+/// ge├ğti─şi i├ğin test ayn─▒ zamanda g├╝venli kopyalama s├Âzle┼şmesini de s─▒nar.
+pub fn test_file_descriptor_lifecycle(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[FD-TEST] --- Dosya tan─▒t─▒c─▒s─▒ ya┼şam d├Âng├╝s├╝ testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let open_before = proc_mgr.files.open_count();
+    let refs_before = proc_mgr.files.reference_count();
+
+    let Some(pid) = proc_mgr
+        .spawn(
+            "fdtest",
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+        .ok()
+    else {
+        crate::serial_print("[FD-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    };
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // 1. 0/1/2 standart tan─▒t─▒c─▒lar─▒ haz─▒r olmal─▒.
+    let std_fds_ok = proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+        p.fds[fd::FD_STDIN].used
+            && p.fds[fd::FD_STDOUT].used
+            && p.fds[fd::FD_STDERR].used
+            && fd::used_fd_count(&p.fds) == 3
+    });
+
+    // 2. Heap'te yol ve okuma tamponu haz─▒rla. `brk` tembel e┼şlendi─şi i├ğin
+    //    sayfa `uaccess` yazm─▒ndan ├Ânce ger├ğekle┼ştirilir (Ring 3 yolu `#PF`).
+    let heap_end = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE, pmm);
+    let heap_base = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|p| p.heap_address(0))
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let path_addr = heap_base;
+    let buffer_addr = heap_base + 128;
+    let path_ok = heap_base != 0
+        && heap_end == heap_base + PAGE_SIZE
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            uaccess::copy_to_user(&p.address_space, path_addr, b"/etc/fd-test\0").is_ok()
+        });
+
+    // 3. open + read: i├ğerik ve imle├ğ ilerlemesi.
+    let fd = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let object = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .and_then(|p| p.fds.get(fd as usize))
+        .filter(|entry| entry.used)
+        .map(|entry| entry.object as usize);
+    let size = object
+        .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.object_size(index))
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let open_ok = fd == fd::FIRST_USER_FD as u64 && size > 0;
+    // G├╝venli kopyalama: NULL ve e┼şlenmemi┼ş kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şaret├ğileri reddedilir.
+    let null_open = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let unmapped_open = syscall_dispatch(
+        sys_nos::SYS_OPEN,
+        heap_base + PAGE_SIZE,
+        0,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let null_read = syscall_dispatch_abi(sys_nos::SYS_READ, fd, 0, 4, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let null_resinfo = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_RESINFO, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let safe_copy_ok = null_open == u64::MAX
+        && unmapped_open == u64::MAX
+        && null_read == u64::MAX
+        && null_resinfo == u64::MAX;
+    let read_count =
+        syscall_dispatch_abi(sys_nos::SYS_READ, fd, buffer_addr, size, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let read_ok = read_count == size
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            uaccess::copy_from_user(&p.address_space, buffer_addr, size as usize)
+                .map(|bytes| bytes.starts_with(b"minOs file descriptor lifecycle test"))
+                .unwrap_or(false)
+        });
+
+    // 4. lseek SET/CUR/END + aral─▒k d─▒┼ş─▒ reddi.
+    let seek_set = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_LSEEK, fd, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let seek_cur = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_LSEEK, fd, 4, 1, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let seek_end = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_LSEEK, fd, (-1i64) as u64, 2, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let seek_over = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_LSEEK, fd, size + 1, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let last_byte = syscall_dispatch_abi(
+        sys_nos::SYS_READ,
+        fd,
+        buffer_addr + 256,
+        1,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    );
+    let last_ok = proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+        uaccess::copy_from_user(&p.address_space, buffer_addr + 256, 1)
+            .map(|bytes| bytes.len() == 1 && bytes[0] == b'\n')
+            .unwrap_or(false)
+    });
+    let seek_ok = seek_set == 0
+        && seek_cur == 4
+        && seek_end == size.saturating_sub(1)
+        && seek_over == u64::MAX
+        && last_byte == 1
+        && last_ok;
+
+    // 5. close: fd serbest kal─▒r, sonraki okuma reddedilir.
+    let close_result = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, fd, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let read_closed = syscall_dispatch_abi(sys_nos::SYS_READ, fd, buffer_addr, 4, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let close_ok = close_result == 0
+        && read_closed == u64::MAX
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| fd::used_fd_count(&p.fds) == 3);
+
+    // 6. Ayn─▒ yol iki kez a├ğ─▒l─▒rsa tek nesne + iki referans.
+    let dup_a = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let dup_b = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let dup_object = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .and_then(|p| p.fds.get(dup_a as usize))
+        .filter(|entry| entry.used)
+        .map(|entry| entry.object as usize);
+    let refs_two = dup_object
+        .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects.get(index))
+        .map(|entry| entry.refs)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let duplicate_open_ok = dup_a == fd::FIRST_USER_FD as u64
+        && dup_b == (fd::FIRST_USER_FD + 1) as u64
+        && refs_two == 2
+        && dup_object
+            .is_some_and(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects[index].path_str() == Some("/etc/fd-test"));
+    let close_dup_b = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, dup_b, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let refs_one = dup_object
+        .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects.get(index))
+        .map(|entry| entry.refs)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let close_dup_a = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, dup_a, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let refs_zero = dup_object
+        .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects.get(index))
+        .map(|entry| entry.refs)
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let duplicate_close_ok =
+        close_dup_b == 0 && refs_one == 1 && close_dup_a == 0 && refs_zero == 0;
+
+    // 7. fork: fd tablosunu devral─▒r ve nesne referans─▒ iki olur.
+    let parent_fd = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let parent_object = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .and_then(|p| p.fds.get(parent_fd as usize))
+        .filter(|entry| entry.used)
+        .map(|entry| entry.object as usize);
+    let child = proc_mgr.fork_process(pid, pmm).ok();
+    let fork_inherit_ok = child.is_some_and(|child_pid| {
+        proc_mgr.get_process(child_pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            p.fds
+                .get(parent_fd as usize)
+                .is_some_and(|entry| entry.used)
+                && p.fds[parent_fd as usize].object as usize == parent_object.unwrap_or(usize::MAX)
+        }) && parent_object
+            .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects.get(index))
+            .is_some_and(|entry| entry.refs == 2)
+    });
+    let mut child_close_ok = false;
+    if let Some(child_pid) = child {
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = child_pid;
+        let closed = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, parent_fd, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+        child_close_ok = closed == 0
+            && proc_mgr
+                .get_process(pid)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.fds[parent_fd as usize].used)
+            && parent_object
+                .and_then(|index| proc_mgr.files.objects.get(index))
+                .is_some_and(|entry| entry.refs == 1);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(child_pid, 0, pmm);
+    }
+    let _ = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, parent_fd, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // 8. exec: t├╝m kullan─▒c─▒ fd'leri kapan─▒r, 0/1/2 yeniden kurulur.
+    let _exec_fd = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let exec_image = crate::mm::loader::AppLoader::create_sample_elf();
+    let exec_result = proc_mgr.exec_process(pid, &exec_image, pmm);
+    let exec_ok = exec_result.is_ok()
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            fd::used_fd_count(&p.fds) == 3
+                && p.fds
+                    .iter()
+                    .skip(fd::FIRST_USER_FD)
+                    .all(|entry| !entry.used)
+                && p.fds[fd::FD_STDOUT].used
+        })
+        && proc_mgr.files.reference_count() == refs_before;
+
+    // 9. fd kotas─▒: limit 4 iken 3 standart + 1 a├ğ─▒k dosya s─▒─şar.
+    let quota_heap = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    // Exec sonras─▒ heap sayfas─▒ tembel e┼şlenir; `copy_to_user` yazabilmesi
+    // i├ğin ├Ânce ger├ğekle┼ştirilir (Ring 3'teki ilk yazma #PF ile ayn─▒ yol).
+    let _ = proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE, pmm);
+    let quota_base = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|p| p.heap_address(0))
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let quota_path_ok = quota_base != 0
+        && quota_heap == quota_base + PAGE_SIZE
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            uaccess::copy_to_user(&p.address_space, quota_base, b"/etc/fd-test\0").is_ok()
+        });
+    let mut quota_ok = false;
+    if quota_path_ok {
+        let saved_limit = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+            let old = p.resources.limits.max_fds;
+            p.resources.limits.max_fds = 4;
+            old
+        });
+        if let Some(saved_limit) = saved_limit {
+            let first = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, quota_base, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let second = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, quota_base, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let closed = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, first, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            quota_ok = first == fd::FIRST_USER_FD as u64 && second == u64::MAX && closed == 0;
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid) {
+                p.resources.limits.max_fds = saved_limit;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let no_leak = terminated
+        && pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before
+        && proc_mgr.files.reference_count() == refs_before
+        && proc_mgr.files.open_count() <= open_before
+        && crate::mm::vmm::shared_frame_count() == 0;
+    if !no_leak {
+        crate::serial_print("[FD-TEST] cleanup-dbg terminated=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(terminated as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" free_before=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(free_before);
+        crate::serial_print(" free_now=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(pmm.free_memory_bytes);
+        crate::serial_print(" refs_before=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(refs_before as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" refs_now=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(proc_mgr.files.reference_count() as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" open_before=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(open_before as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" open_now=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(proc_mgr.files.open_count() as u64);
+        crate::serial_print(" shared=");
+        crate::serial::print_dec(crate::mm::vmm::shared_frame_count() as u64);
+        crate::serial_print("\n");
+    }
+
+    if std_fds_ok
+        && path_ok
+        && open_ok
+        && safe_copy_ok
+        && read_ok
+        && seek_ok
+        && close_ok
+        && duplicate_open_ok
+        && duplicate_close_ok
+        && fork_inherit_ok
+        && child_close_ok
+        && exec_ok
+        && quota_ok
+        && no_leak
+    {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[FD-TEST] open/read/lseek/close, refcount, fork/exec ve fd kotas─▒: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[FD-TEST] fd ya┼şam d├Âng├╝s├╝ testi: BA┼ŞARISIZ (");
+        crate::serial_print(if std_fds_ok { "std " } else { "std! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if path_ok { "path " } else { "path! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if open_ok { "open " } else { "open! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if safe_copy_ok {
+            "safe-copy "
+        } else {
+            "safe-copy! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if read_ok { "read " } else { "read! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if seek_ok { "seek " } else { "seek! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if close_ok { "close " } else { "close! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if duplicate_open_ok {
+            "dup-open "
+        } else {
+            "dup-open! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if duplicate_close_ok {
+            "dup-close "
+        } else {
+            "dup-close! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if fork_inherit_ok { "fork " } else { "fork! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if child_close_ok {
+            "child-close "
+        } else {
+            "child-close! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if exec_ok { "exec " } else { "exec! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if quota_ok { "quota " } else { "quota! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "leak-free" } else { "leak!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+        false
+    }
+}
+
+/// S├╝re├ğ ba┼ş─▒na kaynak limitlerini do─şrular: `RESINFO` raporu, heap/mmap/fd/
+/// ├ğer├ğeve kotas─▒ ve `fork` ile limit miras─▒.
+pub fn test_resource_limits(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[RLIMIT-TEST] --- Kaynak limitleri testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+    let open_before = proc_mgr.files.open_count();
+    let refs_before = proc_mgr.files.reference_count();
+
+    let Some(pid) = proc_mgr
+        .spawn(
+            "rlimit",
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+        .ok()
+    else {
+        crate::serial_print("[RLIMIT-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        return false;
+    };
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = pid;
+
+    // 1. Varsay─▒lan limitler.
+    let defaults_ok = proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+        let limits = p.resources.limits;
+        limits.max_frames == MAX_PROC_FRAMES
+            && limits.max_user_frames == MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES
+            && limits.max_fds == fd::MAX_FDS
+            && limits.max_mmap_bytes == MAX_PROC_MMAP_BYTES
+            && limits.max_heap_bytes == MAX_PROC_HEAP_BYTES
+    });
+
+    // 2. RESINFO: 8 x u64 rapor (limit + kullan─▒m). Tembel heap sayfas─▒ rapor
+    //    hedefi olarak kullan─▒laca─ş─▒ i├ğin ├Ânce ger├ğekle┼ştirilir.
+    let heap_end = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE, pmm);
+    let heap_base = proc_mgr
+        .get_process(pid)
+        .map(|p| p.heap_address(0))
+        .unwrap_or(0);
+    let report_ok = heap_base != 0
+        && heap_end == heap_base + PAGE_SIZE
+        && syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_RESINFO, heap_base, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            uaccess::copy_from_user(&p.address_space, heap_base, 64)
+                .map(|bytes| {
+                    let value = |index: usize| {
+                        let mut raw = [0u8; 8];
+                        raw.copy_from_slice(&bytes[index * 8..index * 8 + 8]);
+                        u64::from_le_bytes(raw)
+                    };
+                    value(0) == MAX_PROC_FRAMES as u64
+                        && value(1) == p.resources.frame_count as u64
+                        && value(2) == MAX_PROC_USER_FRAMES as u64
+                        && value(3) == p.resources.user_frame_count as u64
+                        && value(4) == fd::MAX_FDS as u64
+                        && value(5) == fd::used_fd_count(&p.fds) as u64
+                        && value(6) == MAX_PROC_MMAP_BYTES
+                        && value(7) == 0
+                })
+                .unwrap_or(false)
+        });
+
+    // 3. Heap kotas─▒: limit 2 sayfa iken 3 sayfa reddedilir, 2 sayfa kabul edilir.
+    let heap_limit_ok = {
+        let saved = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+            let old = p.resources.limits.max_heap_bytes;
+            p.resources.limits.max_heap_bytes = 2 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            old
+        });
+        if let Some(saved) = saved {
+            let over = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, 3 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let stayed = proc_mgr
+                .get_process(pid)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.heap_brk == PAGE_SIZE);
+            let within = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, 2 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let grew = proc_mgr
+                .get_process(pid)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.heap_brk == 2 * PAGE_SIZE);
+            // Sayfa 1 tembel; k├╝├ğ├╝ltmede yaln─▒z ger├ğek ├ğer├ğevesi olan sayfa iade edilir.
+            let _ =
+                proc_mgr.handle_lazy_fault(pid, crate::mm::user_mem::HEAP_BASE + PAGE_SIZE, pmm);
+            let shrunk = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_BRK, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid) {
+                p.resources.limits.max_heap_bytes = saved;
+            }
+            over == u64::MAX
+                && stayed
+                && within == heap_base + 2 * PAGE_SIZE
+                && grew
+                && shrunk == heap_base + PAGE_SIZE
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    };
+
+    // 4. mmap kotas─▒ canl─▒ b├Âlge baytlar─▒n─▒ sayar; munmap kotay─▒ geri a├ğar.
+    let mmap_limit_ok = {
+        let saved = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+            let old = p.resources.limits.max_mmap_bytes;
+            p.resources.limits.max_mmap_bytes = 2 * PAGE_SIZE;
+            old
+        });
+        if let Some(saved) = saved {
+            let region = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, 2 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let over = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let unmapped = if region != 0 {
+                syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, region, 2 * PAGE_SIZE, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            };
+            let region2 = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MMAP, PAGE_SIZE, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let unmapped2 = if region2 != 0 {
+                syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_MUNMAP, region2, PAGE_SIZE, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            };
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid) {
+                p.resources.limits.max_mmap_bytes = saved;
+            }
+            region != 0
+                && over == 0
+                && unmapped == 0
+                && region2 != 0
+                && region2 != region
+                && unmapped2 == 0
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    };
+
+    // 5. ├çer├ğeve kotas─▒: mevcut kullan─▒m + 2 ├ğer├ğeve; ├╝├ğ├╝nc├╝ istek reddedilir.
+    let frame_limit_ok = {
+        let state = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+            let old = p.resources.limits.max_user_frames;
+            p.resources.limits.max_user_frames = p.resources.user_frame_count + 2;
+            (old, p.resources.user_frame_count)
+        });
+        if let Some((saved, current)) = state {
+            let first = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_ALLOC_FRAME, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let second = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_ALLOC_FRAME, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let third = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_ALLOC_FRAME, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let free_first = if first != 0 {
+                syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_FREE_FRAME, first, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            };
+            let free_second = if second != 0 {
+                syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_FREE_FRAME, second, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+            } else {
+                u64::MAX
+            };
+            let restored = proc_mgr
+                .get_process(pid)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.resources.user_frame_count == current);
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid) {
+                p.resources.limits.max_user_frames = saved;
+            }
+            first != 0
+                && second != 0
+                && first != second
+                && third == 0
+                && free_first == 0
+                && free_second == 0
+                && restored
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    };
+
+    // 6. fd kotas─▒: limit 4 iken yaln─▒zca bir kullan─▒c─▒ fd'si a├ğ─▒labilir.
+    let fd_limit_ok = {
+        let path_addr = heap_base + 256;
+        let path_ok = heap_base != 0
+            && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+                uaccess::copy_to_user(&p.address_space, path_addr, b"/etc/fd-test\0").is_ok()
+            });
+        if !path_ok {
+            false
+        } else if let Some(saved) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+            let old = p.resources.limits.max_fds;
+            p.resources.limits.max_fds = 4;
+            old
+        }) {
+            let first = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let second = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_OPEN, path_addr, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            let closed = syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_CLOSE, first, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm);
+            if let Some(p) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid) {
+                p.resources.limits.max_fds = saved;
+            }
+            first == fd::FIRST_USER_FD as u64 && second == u64::MAX && closed == 0
+        } else {
+            false
+        }
+    };
+
+    // 7. fork limitleri devral─▒r (son ad─▒m: sonras─▒nda kullan─▒c─▒ belle─şine
+    // yaz─▒lmaz, ├ğ├╝nk├╝ fork sayfalar─▒ COW olarak i┼şaretler).
+    let mut child_pid = None;
+    let inheritance_ok = if let Some(parent_limits) = proc_mgr.get_process_mut(pid).map(|p| {
+        p.resources.limits.max_mmap_bytes = 5 * PAGE_SIZE;
+        p.resources.limits
+    }) {
+        child_pid = proc_mgr.fork_process(pid, pmm).ok();
+        child_pid.is_some_and(|child| {
+            proc_mgr
+                .get_process(child)
+                .is_some_and(|p| p.resources.limits == parent_limits)
+        })
+    } else {
+        false
+    };
+
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    if let Some(child) = child_pid {
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(child, 0, pmm);
+    }
+    let terminated = proc_mgr.terminate(pid, 0, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let no_leak = terminated
+        && pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before
+        && proc_mgr.files.reference_count() == refs_before
+        && proc_mgr.files.open_count() <= open_before
+        && crate::mm::vmm::shared_frame_count() == 0;
+
+    if defaults_ok
+        && report_ok
+        && heap_limit_ok
+        && mmap_limit_ok
+        && frame_limit_ok
+        && fd_limit_ok
+        && inheritance_ok
+        && no_leak
+    {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[RLIMIT-TEST] RESINFO, heap/mmap/fd/├ğer├ğeve kotas─▒ ve fork miras─▒: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[RLIMIT-TEST] kaynak limitleri testi: BA┼ŞARISIZ (");
+        crate::serial_print(if defaults_ok {
+            "defaults "
+        } else {
+            "defaults! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if report_ok { "resinfo " } else { "resinfo! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if heap_limit_ok { "heap " } else { "heap! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if mmap_limit_ok { "mmap " } else { "mmap! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if frame_limit_ok { "frame " } else { "frame! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if fd_limit_ok { "fd " } else { "fd! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if inheritance_ok {
+            "inherit "
+        } else {
+            "inherit! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "leak-free" } else { "leak!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+        false
+    }
+}
+
+/// `SYS_KILL` (kill/pause/resume) do─şrulamas─▒: varl─▒k denetimi, ge├ğersiz
+/// sinyal reddi, `pending_signals` i┼şlenmesi, SIGSTOPÔåÆSuspended, SIGCONTÔåÆReady
+/// ve SIGTERM sonland─▒rmas─▒ (`exit_code = -15`).
+pub fn test_kill_suspend_resume(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[KILL-TEST] --- kill/SIGSTOP/SIGCONT/SIGTERM testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+
+    let pid = match proc_mgr.spawn(
+        "sig_test",
+        crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+        crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+        0,
+        None,
+        pmm,
+    ) {
+        Ok(pid) => pid,
+        Err(_) => {
+            crate::serial_print("[KILL-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+            proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+            return false;
+        }
+    };
+
+    let kill = |sig: u64, proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager| {
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_KILL, pid as u64, sig, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+    };
+
+    // 1. Varl─▒k denetimi ve s─▒n─▒r kontrolleri.
+    let probe_ok = kill(0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_KILL, 9999, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX
+        && syscall_dispatch(sys_nos::SYS_KILL, 0, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX
+        && kill(signals::MAX_SIGNAL + 1, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 2. Fatal-olmayan sinyal pending_signals'e i┼şlenir.
+    let pending_ok = kill(signals::SIGUSR1, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.pending_signals & (1u64 << (signals::SIGUSR1 - 1)) != 0);
+
+    // 3. SIGSTOP: s├╝re├ğ Suspended olur ve zamanlay─▒c─▒ onu se├ğmez.
+    let suspended_ok = kill(signals::SIGSTOP, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Suspended)
+        && proc_mgr.schedule_next() != Some(pid);
+
+    // 4. SIGCONT: Suspended s├╝re├ğ Ready'ye d├Âner.
+    let resumed_ok = kill(signals::SIGCONT, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(pid)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Ready || p.state == ProcessState::Running);
+
+    // 5. SIGTERM: s├╝re├ğ -15 ├ğ─▒k─▒┼ş koduyla sonlan─▒r; ikinci kill reddedilir.
+    let terminated_ok = kill(signals::SIGTERM, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(pid).is_some_and(|p| {
+            p.state == ProcessState::Terminated && p.exit_code == -(signals::SIGTERM as i32)
+        })
+        && kill(signals::SIGTERM, proc_mgr, pmm) == u64::MAX;
+
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let no_leak = pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before;
+
+    if probe_ok && pending_ok && suspended_ok && resumed_ok && terminated_ok && no_leak {
+        crate::serial_print("[KILL-TEST] kill/SIGSTOP/SIGCONT/SIGTERM: BASARILI\n");
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[KILL-TEST] kill/sinyal testi: BA┼ŞARISIZ (probe=");
+        crate::serial_print(if probe_ok { "ok " } else { "! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if pending_ok { "pending " } else { "pending! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if suspended_ok { "stop " } else { "stop! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if resumed_ok { "cont " } else { "cont! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if terminated_ok { "term " } else { "term! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "leak-free" } else { "leak!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+        false
+    }
+}
+
+/// S├╝re├ğ grubu/oturum, CPU yak─▒nl─▒─ş─▒ ve sinyal i┼şleyici syscall'lar─▒n─▒n
+/// (`SYS_SETPGID`/`SYS_GETPGID`/`SYS_SETSID`/`SYS_GETSID`,
+/// `SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY`/`SYS_SCHED_GETAFFINITY`, `SYS_SIGACTION`/
+/// `SYS_SIGPROCMASK`) u├ğtan uca do─şrulamas─▒.
+pub fn test_process_groups_and_signals(
+    proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+    pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager,
+) -> bool {
+    crate::serial_print("[PROC-GROUP-TEST] --- grup/oturum/affinity/sinyal testi ---\n");
+    let caller_pid = proc_mgr.current_pid;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+    let free_before = pmm.free_memory_bytes;
+
+    let spawn = |name: &str, proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager, pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager| {
+        proc_mgr.spawn(
+            name,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_CODE_BASE,
+            crate::mm::loader::USER_STACK_TOP - 16,
+            0,
+            None,
+            pmm,
+        )
+    };
+    let call = |sys_no: u64,
+                arg1: u64,
+                arg2: u64,
+                proc_mgr: &mut ProcessManager,
+                pmm: &mut PhysicalMemoryManager| {
+        syscall_dispatch(sys_no, arg1, arg2, 0, proc_mgr, pmm)
+    };
+
+    let Ok(parent) = spawn("group_parent", proc_mgr, pmm) else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-GROUP-TEST] S├╝re├ğ olu┼şturulamad─▒: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        return false;
+    };
+
+    // 1. spawn: s├╝re├ğ kendi grubunun ve oturumunun lideri olur.
+    let leader_ok = call(sys_nos::SYS_GETPGID, parent as u64, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == parent as u64
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_GETSID, parent as u64, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == parent as u64;
+
+    // 2. fork: alt s├╝re├ğ grup/oturumu ve affinity maskesini devral─▒r.
+    let Ok(child) = proc_mgr.fork_process(parent, pmm) else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-GROUP-TEST] fork: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(parent, 0, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        return false;
+    };
+    let inherit_ok = call(sys_nos::SYS_GETPGID, child as u64, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == parent as u64
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_GETSID, child as u64, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == parent as u64;
+
+    // 3. setsid: ├ğa─ş─▒ran (child) yeni oturum ve grup lideri olur; `pid == 0`
+    //    ├ğa─ş─▒ran─▒ hedefler.
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = child;
+    let session_ok = call(sys_nos::SYS_SETSID, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == child as u64
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_GETPGID, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == child as u64
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_GETSID, 0, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == child as u64
+        // Oturum ├╝yeli─şi: yeni oturumda yaln─▒zca `child`, ebeveyn oturumunda
+        // yaln─▒zca `parent` bulunur.
+        && proc_mgr.kill_session(child, 0, pmm) == 1
+        && proc_mgr.kill_session(parent, 0, pmm) == 1;
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = 0;
+
+    // Farkl─▒ oturumdaki bir gruba ge├ği┼ş reddedilir (POSIX EPERM).
+    let cross_session_rejected = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_SETPGID,
+        child as u64,
+        parent as u64,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 4. CPU yak─▒nl─▒─ş─▒: varsay─▒lan t├╝m CPU'lar, daraltma ve ge├ğersiz maske.
+    let cpus = crate::arch::x86_64::smp::cpu_count().clamp(1, 64) as u32;
+    let all_mask = if cpus >= 64 {
+        u64::MAX
+    } else {
+        (1u64 << cpus) - 1
+    };
+    let affinity_ok = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_GETAFFINITY,
+        parent as u64,
+        0,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == all_mask
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY,
+            parent as u64,
+            1,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_GETAFFINITY,
+            parent as u64,
+            0,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 1
+        && (cpus >= 64
+            || call(
+                sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY,
+                parent as u64,
+                all_mask << 1,
+                proc_mgr,
+                pmm,
+            ) == u64::MAX)
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_SCHED_SETAFFINITY,
+            parent as u64,
+            0,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == u64::MAX;
+
+    // 5. S├╝re├ğ grubuna toplu sinyal: negatif pid ile `SIGSTOP`, ard─▒ndan
+    //    tekil `SIGCONT`. `child` ayr─▒ oturumda oldu─şu i├ğin etkilenmez.
+    let Ok(sibling) = proc_mgr.fork_process(parent, pmm) else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-GROUP-TEST] ikinci fork: BA┼ŞARISIZ\n");
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(child, 0, pmm);
+        let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(parent, 0, pmm);
+        proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+        proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+        return false;
+    };
+    let group_mask = (-(parent as i64)) as u64;
+    let group_stop_ok = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+        group_mask,
+        signals::SIGSTOP,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            child as u64,
+            signals::SIGSTOP,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(parent)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Suspended)
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(sibling)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Suspended)
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(child)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.state == ProcessState::Suspended);
+    let group_resume_ok = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+        parent as u64,
+        signals::SIGCONT,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            sibling as u64,
+            signals::SIGCONT,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            child as u64,
+            signals::SIGCONT,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0;
+
+    // 6. Sinyal i┼şleyicisi: SIGKILL reddedilir, kullan─▒c─▒ i┼şleyicisi bekleyen
+    //    sinyali varsay─▒lan eyleme d├╝┼ş├╝rmez, SIG_IGN sinyali d├╝┼ş├╝r├╝r.
+    //    `SYS_SIGACTION`/`SYS_SIGPROCMASK` ├ğa─ş─▒ran s├╝reci hedefler.
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = parent;
+    let sig_usr1_bit = 1u64 << (signals::SIGUSR1 - 1);
+    let sig_usr2_bit = 1u64 << (signals::SIGUSR2 - 1);
+    let handler_rejected = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_SIGACTION,
+        signals::SIGKILL,
+        0x1234,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == u64::MAX;
+    let handler_ok = call(
+        sys_nos::SYS_SIGACTION,
+        signals::SIGUSR1,
+        0x7000_1234,
+        proc_mgr,
+        pmm,
+    ) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            parent as u64,
+            signals::SIGUSR1,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && !proc_mgr.deliver_pending_defaults(parent, pmm)
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(parent)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.pending_signals & sig_usr1_bit != 0);
+
+    let ignore_ok = call(sys_nos::SYS_SIGACTION, signals::SIGUSR1, 1, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(parent)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.pending_signals & sig_usr1_bit == 0)
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            parent as u64,
+            signals::SIGUSR1,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && proc_mgr.deliver_pending_defaults(parent, pmm)
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(parent).is_some_and(|p| {
+            p.pending_signals & sig_usr1_bit == 0 && p.state != ProcessState::Terminated
+        });
+
+    // 7. Sinyal maskesi: maskeli sinyal varsay─▒lan eyleme d├╝┼şmez; maske
+    //    kald─▒r─▒l─▒nca SIGUSR2 varsay─▒lan eylemi s├╝reci sonland─▒r─▒r.
+    let mask_ok = call(sys_nos::SYS_SIGACTION, signals::SIGUSR2, 0, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_SIGPROCMASK, 1, sig_usr2_bit, proc_mgr, pmm) == 0
+        && call(
+            sys_nos::SYS_KILL,
+            parent as u64,
+            signals::SIGUSR2,
+            proc_mgr,
+            pmm,
+        ) == 0
+        && !proc_mgr.deliver_pending_defaults(parent, pmm)
+        && proc_mgr
+            .get_process(parent)
+            .is_some_and(|p| p.pending_signals & sig_usr2_bit != 0)
+        && call(sys_nos::SYS_SIGPROCMASK, 0, sig_usr2_bit, proc_mgr, pmm) == sig_usr2_bit
+        && proc_mgr.deliver_pending_defaults(parent, pmm)
+        && proc_mgr.get_process(parent).is_some_and(|p| {
+            p.state == ProcessState::Terminated && p.exit_code == -(signals::SIGUSR2 as i32)
+        });
+
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(child, 0, pmm);
+    let _ = proc_mgr.terminate(sibling, 0, pmm);
+    proc_mgr.reap_terminated(pmm);
+    proc_mgr.current_pid = caller_pid;
+    let no_leak = pmm.free_memory_bytes == free_before;
+
+    if leader_ok
+        && inherit_ok
+        && session_ok
+        && cross_session_rejected
+        && affinity_ok
+        && group_stop_ok
+        && group_resume_ok
+        && handler_rejected
+        && handler_ok
+        && ignore_ok
+        && mask_ok
+        && no_leak
+    {
+        crate::serial_print(
+            "[PROC-GROUP-TEST] grup/oturum/affinity/sinyal i┼şleyicileri: BASARILI\n",
+        );
+        true
+    } else {
+        crate::serial_print("[PROC-GROUP-TEST] test: BA┼ŞARISIZ (");
+        crate::serial_print(if leader_ok { "leader " } else { "leader! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if inherit_ok { "inherit " } else { "inherit! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if session_ok { "session " } else { "session! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if cross_session_rejected {
+            "cross "
+        } else {
+            "cross! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if affinity_ok {
+            "affinity "
+        } else {
+            "affinity! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if group_stop_ok { "stop " } else { "stop! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if group_resume_ok { "cont " } else { "cont! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if handler_rejected {
+            "kill-action "
+        } else {
+            "kill-action! "
+        });
+        crate::serial_print(if handler_ok { "handler " } else { "handler! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if ignore_ok { "ignore " } else { "ignore! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if mask_ok { "mask " } else { "mask! " });
+        crate::serial_print(if no_leak { "leak-free" } else { "leak!" });
+        crate::serial_print(")\n");
+        false
+    }
+}
